The Bedraggled Parson's regard burns like sepsis, abyss-deep and incurious.="它在梦中窥见你容颜。执哀者于吾梦中低语，细述你喉颈与鼻梁的轮廓，言说那些我无法参透的奥秘。"衣衫褴褛的牧师投来败血症般灼烧的目光，深渊般深邃，却无探究之意。
"They fix you with unblinking eyes. "On the way, I shall perform an assessment of the port and inform you of any dangers that you might have missed. If there are none, I will remain silent, so as not to trouble you unnecessarily."="它用那双不眨的眼睛注视你："途中，我会对车站进行评估，并对任何你可能忽略的危险作出提醒。如果没有，我将保持沉默，以免打扰到你。"
"They were kind to you," the Bedraggled Parson says, abruptly and without vitriol, only a pale wonder, eyes fever-warm under the wilds of his hair. It is all he extends. \n\nWas he talking about the officials from the Bureau of Entertainments? Or someone else?="他们待你仁慈。"衣衫褴褛的牧师突然说道，不带讥讽，唯有苍白的惊异，乱发下双眼发烫。仅此一句。\n\n是指娱乐局的官员？还是另有所指？
"This is what happens when you ask too many questions," remarks the Supervising Devil.="这就是你提太多问题的后果，"魔鬼监督者冷冷地说。
"Today, we stand before They Who Must Grieve to give praise to their sacrifice. Without them, there would be no joy, no agony against which we might juxtapose the sweetness of our existence. Do not ever forget this. If you forget all else, do not forget this." \n\nHe raises his eyebrows at you. "A pleasure to see a new member. Please join us later, on the beach."="今日我们站在执哀者面前，为其牺牲献上赞颂。若无他们，便无所谓的喜悦，也无法体会那种能与甜美人生相对照的痛苦。切莫忘记这一点。即便你忘记一切，也别忘了这个。" \n\n他扬起眉毛望向你："欢迎新成员。待会儿请来海滩一聚。"
"Tragically, things aren't going to plan. We can't afford another expedition, and so we languish here, making what we can out of what we've been able to find thus far. It will not do."="可悲的是，事情并没有按计划进行。我们没有能力再进行一次探险，所以我们在这里苦苦挣扎，用我们迄今为止所能找到的东西来做我们能做的事。这不可能成功。"
"Try to allow yourself to feel some emotion," prompts the Supervising Deviless.="试着让自己感受一些情绪，"女魔鬼监督者引导道。
"Wait, you're a Stainrod, as in Leadbeater & Stainrod?" demands the crewman. "And you're still taking coins out of our pockets?"\n\nThe Driver shrugs. "Black sheep of the family. We don't talk much, not since the accident. Mother didn't approve of my new career and Father, he's... Well, anyway—" they shuffle the cards, smiling with confidence, "Never bet what you can't afford to lose."="等等, 你是斯坦罗德家族的人？就是那个李德彼得&斯坦罗德?" 列车员质问道。 "那你还跟我们赌钱抢硬币?"\n\n司机耸了耸肩。 "我是家里的宠儿败家子。自从那场事故后，我们就很少交流了。妈妈不赞成我现在的职业，而爸爸，他...好吧，不管怎样—" 司机一边洗牌一边信心满满地笑着, "永远别把你承受不起失去的东西压在赌注上。"
"We await at Wolvesey Station."="我们在伍夫塞站等候。"
"We don't have the resources to mount any more expeditions, so I'm left with what meagre evidence I've been able to gather. It convinces me, for sure, but I can't imagine Summerset will look kindly upon it."="我们已无力组织新的探险，只能依赖我所收集到的那点微薄证据。它对我而言已足够有说服力，但我无法想象夏暮会正眼瞧它。"
"We go to the Throne of the Hour-King."="我们去了时间之王的王座。"
"We go to the messenger-corpse; that squirming ruin of tumbled spires."="我们去了信使尸体，那摇摇欲坠的尖塔废墟。"
"We go to the regent's obelisk, which you have made into a gaudy amusement."="我们去了摄政王的方尖碑，那个被你们变成俗丽娱乐场的地方。"
"We go where roses bloom in the dark."="我们去了玫瑰盛开的黑暗之地。"
"We ignore our dreams too easily," says the Bedraggled Parson, eyes abstract, fixed on a sky obscured by sinew. "We pretend that they are nothing but the brain misfiring, that they are memories poorly assembled. But they are more. They are how the gods speak to us. And They Who Must Grieve, they've sent me signs." \n\nHe is not talking with you, but at you. Does he care that he's near-incomprehensible? His followers nod, entranced. They understand, or do not care that they don't.="世人太轻易忽视梦境。"衣衫褴褛的牧师说着，目光游离于筋肉遮蔽的天穹，"总说不过是神经的错乱，是记忆碎片的拙劣拼凑。但不止于此——此乃神谕之道。执哀者……已向我降下征兆。"\n\n与其说是交谈，不如说是宣讲。他知道自己的话近乎呓语吗？追随者们痴迷颔首，或是真能领会，或是根本不在乎是否听懂。
"We journey to the place of apples, where an old regent's ruins lie."="我们前往苹果之地，那里有一个古老摄政王的遗迹。"
"We journey to your house of invention, near the Ormswold."="我们去往你们的发明之屋，在涡野附近。"
"We return to the smoggy, clanking, singing, stamping, thronging, frantic stew-pot of humanity; the blister of glass and steel in the heart of the Garden."="我们回到烟雾缭绕、叮当作响、歌唱、脚步、拥挤、疯狂的人类大乱炖——花园之心的玻璃与钢铁泡沫。"
"We understand that it can feel demeaning." When he speaks, it is with the resonance of a choir, symphonic and strange. "To do such... menial things. But they are necessary. As breathing is necessary, as light, as air."\n\nHe seems to be acknowledging the tasks he asks of you. This is almost certainly no indication that he intends to remunerate you suitably.="我们明白这像是一种折辱。"他开口时带着唱诗班的和声般共鸣，诡谲如交响，"要你做这些……卑贱之事。但皆为必要。如同呼吸，如同光，如同空气。"\n\n这番说辞似是承认让你做的苦差。但绝不意味着他打算给予相称的报酬。
"When I heard the Court was to be graced by Ephemera, I put myself forward as your guide."="听说法庭要迎来一位浮生灵，我便主动提出担任引导者。"
"When he shall be judged, let him be condemned: and let his prayer become sin. Let his days be few; and let another take his office."="他受审判的时候，愿他出来担当罪名。愿他的祈祷，反成为罪。愿他的年日短少。愿别人得他的职分。"
"Would you say you are feeling more or less conscious of your mortality than when you began this programme?" asks the Supervising Devil.="你认为，与这次改造开始前相比，你对自己的死亡感更强还是更弱？"魔鬼监督者询问道。
"Would you take me to the Leadbeater & Stainrod Nature Reserve? I'd love to see the wilderness. From a safe distance, of course."=“能送我去李德彼特&斯坦罗德自然保护区吗？我想看看荒野——当然要保持安全距离。”
"You broke a rule," says the Conformer. "You should have been more careful."="你违反了规则，"顺应者说，"你应该更小心些。"
"You here for the clocks?" The tired looking farmhand rolls his eyes at you. "Couldn't you have been here sooner? Could've had me another couple of hours in bed."="你是来修时钟的？"一名满脸倦意的农场工对你翻了个白眼，"你就不能早点来吗？我本可以多睡上几个小时。"
"You must go to Lustrum," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要前往伍载镇，"保管故事的园丁说，"在那里再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"You must go to the Most Serene Mausoleum, in Albion," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要前往阿尔比恩的圣宁墓，"保管故事的园丁说，"等你到了那里，再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"You must take this to the Eagle's Empyrean in Eleutheria," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要把它带到厄琉忒里亚的雄鹰之苍天上国，"保管故事的园丁说，"等你到了那里，再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"Your debt is paid," the Inhabitor's voice-jar intones. "If you wish, you may return to your chariot, and the way will open."="汝债已偿，"门槛住民的声音罐低声说道，"若汝有意，可归战车。道途将为汝而启。"
'A Forensic Assessment of the Sun's Remains'=《对太阳遗迹的医学评估》
'A breakthrough! I worked late into the night, burning every candle to keep myself warm  The budget can spare it, and d__n the Bursar. There are gaps between the stars, where stars once were. This must be the origination of the Discordance. Our enemies maneuver, out there in the cold and dark, and conspire against itself. But the Azure, the enemy at the Gate, has not turned its gaze away. I feel its light like breath on my neck, I feel its Law. It hates us. I must warn London to only send astronomers whose usage of hours is moderate in future.'='一个突破！我彻夜苦干，燃尽每一根蜡烛以求取暖。预算还能撑得住，去他的财务主管。星星之间出现了空隙，那里曾有星辰。这一定便是不谐的源头。我们的敌人正在那寒冷黑暗之地演习，阴谋诡计，甚至自相对抗。但那蔚蓝，那门前之敌，尚未移开目光。我能感到它的光，就像呼吸贴近后颈；我能感到它的法则。它憎恨我们。我必须警告伦敦，今后只派那些对时间使用尚属节制的天文学家前来。'
'A small measure of progress was achieved. I fixed the telescope firmly on the Horizon, as I had indicated previous. Much must be left to speculation, but I believe there are commonalities with my observances at Port Avon. A different intent, for sure, but one must wonder, for how long have our microscopic activities been under the lens of minds immeasurably superior to our own?'='取得了一点小小的进展。如先前所言，我已将望远镜牢牢对准了地平线。虽仍需推测，但我相信它与我在埃文村的观测存在某种相似之处。当然，意图或许截然不同，但我们不得不想一想：人类的这些微不足道的活动，到底被远胜于我们的生物监视了多久？'
'A thought that has stricken me. Felled me almost. If the stars in their conjunctions are alliances, if the chain is across as well as above and below, what we have is in fact, a lattice. A matrix, if you will. A web.'='我突然产生了一个几乎让我瘫倒的念头。如果星辰之间的连结是一种联盟，如果巨链贯穿上下，那么我们所拥有的其实是一个格子。或者说，一个矩阵。一个网络。'
'Another discovery! This involved some tricky Weft-work, and a very steady hand. But, examining the constellations as I observed them from what might have been millenia ago, and comparing them with the placement of the rock on Polmear & Plenty's, was instructive. I have consulted geologically minded friends. But the theory that regions of the Wilderness are bounded locuses must now be called into question. Incidentally, I am having regrets regarding the absence of my own soul.'='又有一项发现！这需要些高难度的纬线操作，以及一只极其稳定的手。但我将如今看到的星座与或许是千年前的图像进行比对，再将它们与波尔梅尔与普伦蒂的岩石位置对照，结果极具启发性。我已咨询了几位有地质学背景的朋友。但‘外野的各区域是固定界域’这一理论，现在恐怕要被重新审视了。顺便说一句，我对自己灵魂的缺失感到有些后悔。'
'I have let my crew die, while I lived.'='我让我的列车员死去，而我却活着。
'I have let my crew die, while I lived:' and then their names begin to scrawl themselves over one another like a grisly wordsearch.='我让我的列车员死去，而我却活着：'然后他们的名字开始像一个可怕的字谜一样互相涂抹。
'I have lost count of how many of my crew have died. I cannot remember their faces.' Then the names of the crew that have died under your command begin to sear themselves into your skin, in a dense scribble.='我已经记不清楚我的列车员死了多少人了。我不记得他们的脸。然后在你的指挥下死去的列车员的名字开始在你的皮肤上烙印，密密麻麻的涂鸦。
'I was commended at graduation for my tenacity, my nerve, and my lack of imagination. I find myself returning to those virtues more and more lately. I have ordered the telescope directed entirely at the azure. We feel the full force of its glare. I see shades, spirits, phantasms. Sometimes, I think I am being toyed with. Just the other night, I awoke to a beam of blue starlight, right over the scope. I crept out there, and stood, alone, on the edge of night. I felt judgement. All that I am could be boiled to atoms and scattered to the farthest edges of the horizon, and still it would pursue me until not even the wind dares howl my name. And once I am less than the smallest molecule of dust, it would forget me utterly, its attention fixed on some grander design. This is the nature of our enemy.'='毕业时我因坚韧、勇气与缺乏想象力而获赞。这些美德，近来我愈发依赖。我已下令将望远镜完全对准蔚蓝。我们正承受它目光的全然注视。我看见影子、幽魂、幻灵。有时，我觉得自己成了玩物。就在前几晚，我被一道蓝色星光唤醒，正投射在镜筒上。我悄悄走出，独自站在夜之边缘。我感受到审判。我的存在可被分解为最细微的粒子，四散至天边尽头，而它仍会追逐我，直至连风都不敢再呼唤我的名字。待我连尘埃都不如时，它便会彻底遗忘我，将注意力投向更宏大的计划。这，便是我们的敌人之本质。'
'Incandescent fire on the horizon. A warning? A welcome? Sometimes I think they speak in sigils to confound us. Dolton has proved useless, as ever. Perhaps I shall make a pyre of his books and use the ashes to draw the Correspondence in my notes. All this is to say, the heavens remain an enigma, searing, splendid, and sibylline.'='地平线上燃起炽白的火焰。是警告？还是欢迎？有时我觉得它们用符印说话只是为了迷惑我们。多尔顿一如既往地毫无用处。或许我该把他的书烧成灰，然后用灰烬在我的笔记中画信符。总而言之，苍穹仍是谜团，炽烈、瑰丽而晦涩。'
'Nothing to report. Another day twiddling my thumbs. Hurry up and get me a permit from the Ministry if you want to actually learn something, you idle sot. Yours, the Professor.'='无事可报。又是无所事事的一天。你这懒鬼，要是真想学点东西，就赶快去从公德部那里给我弄份许可来。此致，教授。'
'Outrage! Calumny! Perfidy! These were the phrases that stood out most in the Bursar's Annual Report. I had some choice words for him too, as you can well imagine. It is ridiculous that we know so little about our own territories. Their former lords may be vanquished and cast down, but their effects are as tangible as the distant starlight that dapples the boughs of Traitor's Wood. They think me eccentric, but I find their ignorance mad. Why watch the borders when we have no idea what creeps about inside our walls?'='丑闻！中伤！背叛！这些词在书记员的年度报告中最为显眼。我当然也有些妙语送给他，正如你所想象的那样。我们对自己领地的了解如此之少，简直荒谬。那些昔日的领主也许已被击败、被驱逐，但他们留下的影响依旧真实可感，如同叛徒林枝头上斑驳的星光。他们觉得我是个怪人，但我倒觉得他们的无知才是真正的疯狂。为何要监视边境，而我们却连自己墙内潜伏着什么都毫不知情？'
'Progress! Slow and inexorable as the spinning of a spider's web, but progress all the same. A stray observation based on the patches of luminosity in the sky, and the correspondent darkness between others. The constellations are politic.'='有进展了！就像蜘蛛结网一样缓慢而不可阻挡，但进展毕竟是进展。一次偶然的观测记录了天空中明亮斑块的分布，以及其间对应的黑暗。星座之中存有政治。'

'The work continues sporadically. It would help if London would put a bit more support behind me, rather than casting its nets where-ever it thinks it might get a bite. 'There are old prayers. I was thinking about this the other night, when I was at the telescope, alone. The other lazy blighters were long abed. And I was gazing up, where the stars lie enmeshed in their webs, like flies spun in silk, but who eats them? What spiders might crawl on what terrible legs and creep about the heavens, dangling on Correspondence like thread, inching closer and closer to their incandescent meat. But, of course, there are no such spiders. Unless they were introduced. And how do spiders breed, why, they lay eggs! A second thought: to whom, in this event, would the spiders pray?'='我知道一些古老的祷文。前些夜里我独自在望远镜前时想到的——其他那群懒骨头早早就去睡了。我一个人仰望星空，那些星辰被困在网中，如同蚊蝇缠绕于丝中。但又是谁吃掉它们？是哪些蜘蛛，长着可怖的腿，在天穹之上蠕行，借着信符的蛛丝悬挂前行，缓缓靠近它们那炽热的肉食？当然了，这世上是没有这种蜘蛛的……除非，它们是被引入的。蜘蛛如何繁殖？没错，它们产卵！第二个念头是：若真如此，这些蜘蛛又会向谁祷告？'

A moment of hesitation, and then a dam bursts in your mind. Words flood the page and carry you forward in an irresistible current, until you are flung into an ending as though dashed upon rocks. The result is rushed and imperfect. Grimly, you go back. You knock the story down and rebuild it, brick by painstaking brick, working with the nihilist fury of a master blacksmith forging his own guillotine. Finally, just as you are ready to throw your entire manuscript into the fire, you realise that you no longer need to – there's nothing left to fix. The Sixth Canto is complete.=片刻的犹豫后，你脑海中的闸门猛然打开。文字如洪流般席卷纸张，推动你不断向前，直至你被抛入一个突如其来的结局，如同被摔碎在礁石上。结果仓促而不完美。你咬牙回头，从头打碎重建，一字一句，如同一个狂热的工匠在锻造自己的断头台。终于，就在你几乎准备将整个手稿投入火中时，你意识到——已经无可再改。第六章完成了。

After the Titanians have all filtered in, the rules are set: every half hour, each participant will be given a glass of wine. If they do not finish it by the time the next round is served, they are disqualified. The last one remaining wins. What happens next, however, is uncertain. The identity of the winner is a mystery, as is the identity of the one who stole the Empress' bust from its glass case. As a point in fact, the whole week afterward is blurry. But you're certain it was a fine time, after the headaches end.=在狄坦妮娅人全部过滤完毕后，规则就定下来了：每隔半小时，每个参与者将得到一杯酒。如果他们在下一轮酒上桌时没有喝完，就会被取消资格。最后剩下的人获胜。然而，接下来会发生什么，是不确定的。赢家的身份是一个谜，就像从玻璃柜中偷出皇后半身像的人的身份一样。事实上，之后的整个一周都是模糊的。但你可以肯定那是一段美好的时光，在令人头疼的结束之后。
After the Titanians have all filtered in, you are allowed a great honour: to stand on the bar, lay out the rules, and - by your sole discretion - decide the victors. Your arbitration bolsters some and destroys others. But truly, most of them should feel no shame: the quality of the lyrics they fish from their mugs is far superior to what one hears in London. An enclave of the dead. These shades are engaged in rites to prepare their passage through Death's Door. They produce slow, ceremonial music with chimes and drums. A handful of them dance ecstatically, their exertions incongruous with the solemn melody.\n\nWhen you approach, they stop. The laws of guest-right require that they offer you respite.=一块属于死者们的飞地。这些遗魂们为准备他们通向死亡之门的路途举行着仪式。他们演奏着迟缓而夹杂钟声鼓声的仪式音乐。他们中的一些欣喜若狂地跳舞，努力使自己与庄严肃穆的旋律不相称。\n\n当你悄然接近后，他们停下了。游客的权利法规要求他们为你提供休憩的条件。
An enclave of the dead. These shades – some once human, some not, all masked and quiet – are performing the observances they hope will secure their passage through Death's Door. \n\nThey pray around a pattern of stones arrayed in complex circles. Occasionally, one of them stands and moves a stone, provoking a fierce, but silent and gesticulatory, argument.\n\nWhen you approach, they stop. The laws of guest-right require that they offer you respite.=一块属于死者们的飞地。这些遗魂们–无论曾经是否为人，皆脸着假面，安静肃穆，举行着他们希望能够在前往死亡之门的路上保护他们的仪式。\n\n他们身披错综复杂的环圈，围绕在一些石头而成的图案旁祈祷。偶尔，他们中的一员起身并移动石块，凶狠地挑衅，但终究沉默，以手语争吵着。\n\n当你悄然接近后，他们停下了。游客的权利法规要求他们为你提供休憩的条件。

As a co-founder, your arrival is treated with the pomp normally afforded to a town mayor or local celebrity. Caps are doffed. Forelocks tugged. The Insistent Surveyor will not let you leave until you've shared afternoon tea.=作为联合创始人，你受到的礼遇堪比镇长或本地名流。工人们纷纷脱帽致意。执着的测量员坚持要你共进下午茶后才肯放行。

As the demons vanish, you find yourself transported back to the Castle of the Seraphim. But all is not well. \n\nThe knights have taken off their friendly faces, revealing warty flesh and sharp teeth. They must be demons themselves, or at least in allegiance with demons. One of them is approaching you, a sizzling-red poker held aloft, ready to put out your eyes and finish the Throne's work.=当恶魔消失，你发现自己又回到了炽天使之城堡。但情况并未得到改善。\n\n骑士们摘下了他们友善的面具，露出遍是凸起的皮肤与尖锐的牙齿。他们必定是恶魔，或至少与恶魔同流。其中一个正向你逼近，手里高举着一根灼热发红的烙铁，准备完成盲目王座未竟的工作，夺走你的双眼。

The stoker falters, then drops their wrench, eyes brimming. \n\nSobbing, the stoker staggers towards the hatch, and hurls themselves out into the Wilderness, tears freezing upon their cheeks.=当司炉工逼近时，诗意的锻造同伴吟诵了一首关于自己即将死去的挽歌。司炉工踉跄停下，手中的扳手跌落，眼中满是泪光。\n\n司炉工啜泣着，摇摇晃晃地走向舱口，将自己投向荒野，泪水在脸颊上结成冰霜。
As the stoker advances, the Studious Forged Companion explains why the stoker would be wrong to kill them. The Companion's logic is precise, and the scope of their argument vast. The stoker staggers into a chair, eyes glazed. "I never thought—" they say in a cracked voice, "—but you're right. It is all connected. Everything."\n\nDays later, the stoker, who has not eaten or slept since, is still slumped in their chair, their flesh grey. They are dead.=当司炉逼近时，好学的锻造同伴解释为何杀死它是错误的决定。它的逻辑严谨，论证范围之广令人惊愕。司炉踉跄地跌坐在椅中，眼神空洞。“我从没想过——”这人声音干裂地低语，“——但你说得对。这一切，都是相互关联的。”\n\n数日之后，那位司炉依然瘫坐在椅上，未曾进食也未曾入眠。其肌肤变得灰白。这人已经死了。

As you enter, the Strongwoman puts down a romance novel which fell out of vogue decades ago. She bids you take a seat, and makes a few efforts at polite conversation; her interest in the weather, though, is obviously feigned. \n\nShe sighs. "I beg your pardon. I find myself distracted, sometimes. I've never come to terms with the rhythms of life here. People show interest, bring interesting things for me to work with; and then they stop."=你进入时，大力女放下了一本早在几十年前便已不流行的言情小说。她请你落座，并努力展开几句客套的寒暄——但她对天气的兴趣明显是装出来的。\n\n她叹了口气："请原谅，我有时心神不定。我一直难以适应这里的生活节奏。人们曾经对我感兴趣，带来各种新奇的东西让我施展才能……然后就没了下文。"
As you finish your work, the Khan's enforcers are wasting no time tracking down London diplomats whose domicile rights have expired. The richest are welcomed to settle the matter with the currency of their choice. The rest soon find their visas claimed by the Eagle, and are deported on the first available trains with little but the clothes on their backs, and no thought given to the damage to the London Embassy's schedules.=当你完成工作时，汗国的执法者毫不犹豫地开始追捕居住权已过期的伦敦外交官。最富有的那些人可以用金钱来摆平此事。其余的人很快发现他们的签证已被雄鹰汗收回，只能带着身上的衣物，乘坐最近一班火车驱逐出境，连伦敦大使馆日程的影响也顾不上了。
As you finish, a Dignified Gentleman of Leisure passes by, back from several months spent circumnavigating the known Wilderness. "Have no fear, gentle Pasporto. When I made my grand wager, I was sure to factor in that New Winchester is typically a full day behind Albion time. We have-" He looks up at the clock and falls to his knees. "No!" he screams. As you finish, the Murderous Sort in the court looks up at the clock with a smug expression. "Where was I at the time of the murders, mi'lud? Why, I will be right here, mi'lud. And so will all of you." By law, the alibi stands.=当你检查完时钟，法庭上的凶手抬头看着钟，脸上露出得意的表情。"法官大人，谋杀案发生时我人在哪儿？看看表吧，等谋杀案发生时，我就待在这里，法官大人，和你们待在一起。"根据法律，不在场证明成立了。
As you kneel, the rodents form a semi-circle around you. One of them squeaks, runs a little way, then runs back and squeaks again.\n\nFinally you relent, and follow it between several mounds of rubbish. It stops beside the small corpse of some unidentifiable creature that looks like a cross between worm and crab.\n\nWhatever this is, you've never seen it anywhere else. It might be a monster unique to London's undercarriage. Probably best to take it back to the engine for study.=当你跪下时，那些啮齿动物围成了一个半圆。其中一只吱吱叫着跑出几步，又跑回来再叫了一声。\n\n最终你妥协了，跟着它穿过几堆垃圾山。它停在一具小小的尸体旁——那是一种无法辨认的生物，看起来像是蠕虫与螃蟹的杂交。\n\n不管这是什么，你从未在别处见过。它可能是伦敦地底下独有的怪物。最好还是把它带回列车上研究一下。
As you leave, your job complete, a small riot is breaking out. It would be larger, but most of the workers are too exhausted and in need of a little rest and recreation. Your adjustments should ensure their working conditions remain arduous rather than torturous, at least for a while.=当你完成工作离开时，一场小规模的骚乱正在爆发。若非大多数工人已疲惫不堪，急需些许休息与消遣，动乱本可能更为激烈。你的调整应该能确保在一段时间内，他们的工作强度能被称作艰苦，而非全然折磨的程度。
As you move through the passage, assaulted by the unholy clamour of the hour-looms, it is impossible to deny the grandiosity of Perdurance. Or its mechanised horror.=穿行于走廊间，时织机那渎神的轰响从四面八方侵扰着你。琥珀宫的恢弘是不容置疑的——它的机械恐怖同样如是。
As you pass the small schoolhouse, a bored child slumps at an uncomfortable wooden desk. Just another ten minutes until freedom. Five minutes. But no. Suddenly the hands on the port clock begin turning backwards; the afternoon and the morning peeling away hour by hour. The Stern Schoolmistress raps her cane against the table. "Enough! Back to the start of today's lesson..."=当你走过一所小学校时，一个百无聊赖的孩子瘫坐在一张硬邦邦的木桌旁。再过十分钟就要下课了，再过五分钟就好了，但是最终没有下课。突然，车站时钟的指针开始逆时针旋转，时间倒退了一个又一个小时，从下午回到了早上。严厉的女校长用手杖敲着桌子。"安静！回到今天的课刚开始的地方……"
As you return from the clock, you pass an Usurious Landlord waving his chalk at a desperate family. "Three of the clock, I said, and not a second later! Is it my fault that the clocks today are as reliable as your word? Humbug! To the poorhouse with the lot of you!"=当你检查完时钟，你与一个放高利贷的房东擦肩而过，他正冲着一家绝望的人挥舞着粉笔。"我说过，三点整，一秒钟也不多等！今天的表就和你那时的话一样信不过，但这是我的错吗？满嘴谎话！你们这帮人都给我滚去救济院吧！"
As you return from your duties, a Windward Inspector is smiling unpleasantly at the Captain from Lustrum. "Due this morning, these were. If you didn't want your kids starving, should've stuck to your schedule." He sucks happily on a boiled sweet. "Shall we start the bribery at 20 per cent?"=当你完成任务返回时，一名迎风公司的检察官正带着不怀好意的笑容看着来自伍载镇的车长。"这些货物今早就该送到了。如果不想让你的孩子挨饿，你本该按时送达。"他自得地含着一颗糖果。"咱们的贿赂就从20\u0025开始谈起吧？"
As you step inside the Court you are immediately accosted by another skull-headed gentleman, though his ivory is noticeably more worse for wear than the Administrator's: yellowing, cracked, pitted with craters.=当你踏入法庭，一位头颅为骨的绅士立刻拦住了你，不过与管理员相比，他的头骨更显破败：泛黄、龟裂、布满坑洞。
As you step inside the Court you are met, not by one of the skull-headed Graven, but by a spirit in a porcelain mask. A voice issues from the jar slung across its back. It introduces itself as the Exacting Ombudsman.=当你走进法庭，迎接你的不是那些头颅为骨的墓族，而是一个戴着瓷面具的鬼魂。一个声音从它背后悬挂的罐子中传出，自称是严苛的监察员。
As you step into the snow, the beings are waiting for you. They press against you and your crew, escorting you to the altar. Their feathered cloaks are down-soft; their strength is pointedly restrained.=当你踏入雪地时，这些存在正等待着你。它们簇拥着你和你的同伴，护送你们走向祭坛。它们的羽制斗篷如绒毛般柔软；刻意收敛着力量。
As you walk down a crowded thoroughfare, you notice the masked spirits shuffling to one side and dropping reverently to their knees. Some are raising unlit candles; some have retrieved broken hourglasses from their robes. Others are scrawling complex symbols on the floor in chalk.=当你经过一条拥挤的街道，你看到覆着假面的鬼魂蹒跚着向一边走去，虔诚的跪下。有些捧着未点燃的蜡烛；有些试图倒转礼袍上的破碎沙漏。其余的则潦草的在地上用粉笔涂鸦着复杂的符号。

The Conformer strides ahead, carefully pointing out where you must duck your head or watch your step.=皮拉内西内部自是比外部更广阔。顺应者的灯光只能照亮有限的范围——你瞥见令人眩晕的螺旋阶梯，缠绕铁链的石柱，墙面上刻有水流的痕迹。顺应者小心翼翼地走在前方，不时提醒你低头或注意脚下。
Piranesi is, of course, bigger on the inside. The Deformer's lamplight stretches only so far; you catch sight of dizzying spiral stairways, columns clad in chains, walls grooved by trickling water. The Deformer lurches ahead, breath steaming in the cold air.=皮拉内西内部自是比外部更广阔。易形者的灯光只能照亮有限的范围——你瞥见令人眩晕的螺旋阶梯，缠绕铁链的石柱，墙面上刻有水流的痕迹。易形者踉跄前行，呼吸在冷空气中结出雾气。
Piranesi is, of course, bigger on the inside. The Performer's lamplight stretches only so far; you catch sight of dizzying spiral stairways, columns clad in chains, walls grooved by trickling water. The Performer scampers ahead, chattering idly about the streets and architecture of London.=皮拉内西内部自是比外部更广阔。表演者的灯光只能照亮有限的范围——你瞥见令人眩晕的螺旋阶梯，缠绕铁链的石柱，墙面上刻有水流的痕迹。表演者一路蹦跳前行，随意地聊起伦敦的街道与建筑。
Piranesi is, of course, bigger on the inside. The Reformer's lamplight stretches only so far; you catch sight of dizzying spiral stairways, columns clad in chains, walls grooved by trickling water. Port Avon nestles, quietly, among ruins wrought by giants. The breeze nudges the apple trees. Locals watch you pass. Their nods are polite. Their eyes are wary.=埃文村静静地依偎在由巨人锻造的建筑的废墟中。微风吹拂着苹果树。当地人看着你经过。他们的点头致意是客气的。他们的眼中神色是警惕的。
Port Avon spills, prettily, across sections of colossal, drifting ruins. Its orchards bask in the evening-gold light of a distant nebula.=埃文村在眼前优美地铺展开来，跨过一段段庞大的、浮动的废墟。它的果园沐浴在远方星云投射出的晚霞般的金光中。
Port Avon's picture-box cottages, each painted a different colour, tuck amidst the great stone ruins. The dock bustles; the lanes throng with visitors.=埃文村里五颜六色的画框小屋挤在巨大的石头废墟之间。码头上熙熙攘攘，小巷里挤满游客。

Puck's Corner is a small park where speech is free and unrestricted, though louder mouths should take care to remember that what they say isn't forgotten when they climb down from the literal soapbox. For the most part it is a place to tell stories rather than air grievances, with poets and comedians testing out new material free booing or ballistic tomatoes.=帕克之角是一座小型公园，在这里，言论自由不受限制。但口若悬河者须谨记：当你从字面意义上的“肥皂箱”上下台后，你所说的话依旧会被铭记。总体而言，这里更像是说书人的乐园，而非抱怨者的角落——诗人、喜剧演员可在此测试新作，不必担心嘘声或迎面飞来的番茄。
Puck's Corner is ready to hear any opinions.=帕克之角已准备好聆听各方论点。

The Erlking's Head is a poet's interpretation of a traditional Albion pub. From the outside, it looks like any other. The architecture seems to have changed since you last walked here, but it's as jumbled and oppressive as ever.=油滑的易形者在你前方疾行，一边把兜帽拉好。穿过狭窄的楼梯，跨过摇摇欲坠的桥梁，走过崩塌的垛口。建筑仿佛和你上次来时不一样了，但依旧混乱而压抑。
The Grand Tournament stretches over countless acres of mud and filth. The air is filled with sobbing, incoherent shouting, screams; occasionally, the rhythmic thud of fists hitting flesh.\n\nThere are hundreds of questers wandering back and forth in the desolation, some more lucid than others. Most are miserable specimens: bleary-eyed, shivering, wearing threadbare rags. You pass one on his hands and knees, furiously gnawing at a freshly-caught rat and complimenting a non-existent chef.=大骑士会武在无边的泥泞与污秽之中开展。空气中弥漫着抽泣、语无伦次的喊叫与尖叫声，偶尔还有拳头击打肉体的有节奏闷响。\n\n数百名探寻者在这片荒地中来回踱步，有些神志尚清，有些则早已迷失。大多数人形容憔悴、眼神呆滞，瑟缩着身子穿着破旧的布片。你经过一位正趴在地上啃咬刚抓到的老鼠的男子，他还一边赞美着一位并不存在的厨师。
The Grand Tournament stretches over countless acres of the Queen's gardens. The air is filled with song, laughter, the roaring of crowds; occasionally, the clash and clang of weaponry. Cooks are working tirelessly in the open air, roasting entire pigs on spits, mixing spices, baking crusty breads, and generally filling the air with a barrage of mouth-watering scents. \n\nThe happy atmosphere among the spectators belies the tension among the knights. Each quester is determined to triumph and take up the completed Minute to Midnight.=大骑士会武占据了女王花园内无尽的土地。空气中回荡着歌声、笑声和人群的欢呼，间或传来武器碰撞的铿锵之音。厨师们在露天辛勤忙碌，整头猪在火上旋转烤制，香料翻飞，硬壳面包出炉，空气中充斥着令人垂涎欲滴的香气。\n\n观众之间洋溢着欢乐气氛，但参赛骑士之间却暗潮汹涌。每位探寻者都决意夺得胜利，拿起那把完整的午夜来临之刻。
The Grave is empty and silent as death. Whatever once dwelt here does no longer.=墓穴空无一物，静寂如死。曾居于此者已不复存在。

The Great Exhibition takes up an entire dome, offering visitors a look at all the wonders of both London and the High Wilderness. Steam engines rumble and hoot. Stolen electrical devices from the Empyrean spray lightning into the atmosphere. Strange ambers of the Rubbery Men sit under spotlights, casting hypnotic colours as they slowly melt in the heat.=大展会占据了一整座穹顶，向来访者展现伦敦与天野中的奇迹。蒸汽引擎轰鸣着、长鸣着；从苍天上国偷来的电器将雷电喷洒至空气中；软体人带来的奇异琥珀在聚光灯下熔化，投射出让人昏沉的色彩。
The Great Library is open.=大图书馆现已开放。
The Great Library of Titania is mostly empty shelves, the original settlers having brought few books with them. With each new arrival, however, shelves begin filling with essential texts, including volumes Q–T of the Britannica, the almost complete works of Shakespeare, and a rare copy of the 1631 'Wicked Bible' containing the infamous misprint 'thou shalt commit adultery'.=狄坦妮娅大图书馆中的书架大多空空如也——最初的定居者带来的书籍寥寥无几。然而随着每一位新来者的抵达，书架逐渐被各类重要文本填满，例如《大英百科全书》第Q至T卷、莎士比亚的几乎全部作品，以及一册罕见的1631年版“罪恶圣经”，该版本中误植了臭名昭著的戒律：“你应当犯奸淫。”

The Heiress won't leave without her sister. Find the Counsellor's agent at Perdurance and seek advice.=女继承人要求和她姐姐一起离开。找到恐怖顾问的密探并寻求建议。
The Hermit accepts your offering and gestures for you to join them; to soothe the dying animal's pain in its last moments. They sift their fingers through its insides, plucking out organs and placing them on the ground in a careful but indecipherable pattern. When it is complete, the Hermit rests a compassionate hand on the beast's head and slits its throat in a smooth, practised manner. What they hoped to learn from this goes unspoken, but as you stand to leave, you see the pattern. An eye.=隐士接受你的提议，打手势示意让你加入，来减轻濒死动物在最后时刻的痛苦。他用手指在动物的体内筛选，取出器官，以一种小心但晦涩难明的图案放在地上。完成后，隐士同情的将手放在野兽的头上，并以平稳、老练的方式切开它的喉咙。他希望从中学习的东西并未说明，但当你站起准备离开时，你看到了图案——一只眼睛。
The Hermit cups their hands beneath a gentle trickle of foul smelling water. They drink, then their words flow freely. They speak of the great charnel houses of Europe. How the Eagle will fight the Bear. How the Clockwork God will fall. A whisper of your own future too, never intended for your ears. A warning of what you may become in the dark.=隐士把手放在一股散发着恶臭的细流下。他只有饮下流水，才能连贯自由的说话。他谈及欧洲最大的埋骨地，鹰如何与熊搏斗，发条之神因何陨落。一个关于你自己未来的耳语，从未想过会出现在你的耳边；一个关于你在黑暗中可能会变成什么的警告。
The Hermit cuts a purple flower from their shoulder, licking the blood from the stem. They wrap it tight and place it in an old chipped cup full of gathered rainwater, stirring with the obsidian shard until it runs dark. They offer it. There is no refusing. When you wake up, only blessed fragments of nightmare remain. Burning at their core is an eye; the same one you now see repeated again and again in the Hermit's garden of flesh.=隐士从他的肩膀上割下一朵紫色的花，舔着茎上的血。他把它包得紧紧的，放在一个有缺口的旧杯子里，杯子里装满了收集的雨水，用黑曜石细条搅拌，直到天黑。他提供这个，无法拒绝。当你醒来时，唯有噩梦仍存。在他的心脏中燃烧着的是一只眼睛，那只你在隐士的血肉之园上反复看到的。
The Hermit drags a sharp rock through their flesh, carving spiral patterns on already bloodied skin. Their milky-grey eyes stare at something beyond the cave wall. Their whispers could as easily be prophecy or madness.=隐士拉拽着一块锋利的石片穿过他的身体，在已经鲜血淋漓的皮肤上雕刻出螺旋式的图案。他乳灰色的眼睛似乎盯着洞穴外的什么东西。他的低语似乎能轻易做出预言，抑或是招致疯狂。
The Hermit finishes carving their latest spiral and takes a drink from a gentle trickle of water running down from the roof of the cave. Their milky-grey eyes stare past you; their mouth occasionally forms a coherent word. Sweat builds on their brow. Their words might be truth, falsehood, or any flavour of madness in between.=隐士完成了他手上的螺旋图案的雕刻，并啜饮从洞穴顶部缓缓流下的细流。他乳白色的灰色眼睛凝视着你，偶尔说些什么。汗水积聚在他们的额头上。他的话可能是真的，也可能是假的，抑或是介于两者之间的呢喃疯语。
The Hermit lingers at the edge of sight, their rags the remnants of a zee-captain's uniform. Two of their eyes are closed tight. The third, carved with scars into their arm, seems to stare through you.=隐士徘徊在视界的边缘，他身上的破布是海泽船长制服的残余部分。他双眼紧闭，而刻满疤痕的手臂上的第三只眼睛好似紧紧注视着你。
The Hermit sits motionless, meditating under a steady drip of water that feeds their garden of flesh. Their unblinking eyes pay you no attention as you take the candle deeper into the cave. Roses bloom in the dark, curling up and dying in the presence of the light.=隐士一动不动地坐着，在源源不断的水滴下冥想，这滴水滋养了他的血肉之园。当你持着蜡烛深入洞穴时，他那不眨的双眼没有注意到你。玫瑰在黑暗中绽放，却光明面前蜷缩着枯萎。
The Hermit whispers to the darkness. They pay you no mind as you look at their few remaining possessions. A trading log from a place far away, a faded photo of a love last seen in Old London. A diary that begins with scholarly descriptions of Eleutherian wonders and ends with nothing but scribbles of the same eye carved into their flesh. The last coherent entry is dated November 1886. =隐士对着黑暗低语，并不在乎你看到他仅剩的资产——一份遥远地区的交易日志，一张在旧伦敦最后一次与爱人相见所拍的褪色的照片。一份以厄琉忒里亚景色的学术性描述为起点，以胡乱涂鸦其身体上镌刻的眼睛为结尾。最后一个连贯的条目日期是1886年11月。
The Hermit works their haruspicy on the dying beast, occasionally pausing to stroke its fur and close its eyes to the pain. Their cave is filled with bones and rotting meat and a smell that makes your eyes water. Amongst them is the wreckage of what appears to be a zubmarine. A second glance at the bones reveals that not all of them are from animals.=隐士在濒死的野兽上实施他的脏卜术, 偶尔停下来抚摸它的皮毛，使它在疼痛中闭上眼睛。他的洞穴里满是骨头和腐烂的肉，还有一种让你流眼泪的气味。其洞穴大致是一艘潜艇的残骸，再看一眼这些骨头，就会发现并非所有骨头都来自动物。
The Hermit's puckered skin is a mass of fungal blooms and the purple-leafed flowers of twilight. They tend to them with a sliver of obsidian, delicately carving weeds from their flesh.=隐士那皱缩的皮肤是由一团团菌落般的，在暮色映衬下叶色艳紫的花朵相接而成。他乐于以黑曜石的细条在血肉上精心雕刻，去除芜杂。
The Hermit's words are rapid in pace and low on coherence. They speak while painting a picture on the wall; another eye, like the one in their tattoo. Now your vision has adjusted, just a little, to the darkness, you can see the same symbol emblazoned a thousand times across the walls, sometimes open, often closed. Just for a moment, you understand. Just for a moment, you see.=隐士的话语节奏快却不连贯，他一边在墙上画画一边说话，而另一只眼睛，就像他们纹身上的那只。你的视力稍微适应了黑暗，可以在墙上看到同样的符号，其中有些是开放的，有些是闭合的。仅一会儿，你看到了，仅一会儿，你理解了。

The Queen calls up her seneschal and hands him a scroll. "At last," mutters the seneschal. "The Grand Tournament. We've been waiting for years. How long do I have to make preparations?"\n\n"The Tournament will begin in two weeks," says the Queen. The seneschal stares at her in abject horror, then turns on his heels and begins shrieking instructions to his attendants.=女王召来她的总管，并递给他一卷羊皮纸卷。"终于，"总管喃喃道，"我们等待多年的大骑士会武终于要来了。我有多少时间可以准备？"\n\n"会武将在两周后开始。"女王说道。总管瞪大了眼，满脸惊恐地盯着她，然后猛地转身，对侍从们尖声下达命令。
The Queen hesitates, clasping her hands under her chin.=女王迟疑了一下，将双手合十，轻轻放在下巴下。
The Queen stares down at you in horror. The moon vanishes from the sky, and you find yourself back beneath the gathering storm.\n\n"Why – why would you—" The Unseen Queen is fading. The very fact of her existence is still too fragile to cope with the Minute to Midnight's onslaught of weaponised time.\n\nAs she convulses, the Storm That Speaks swells, gathers strength. It seems it wasn't quite yet destroyed; and now it senses weakness in its would-be conqueror.=女王惊恐地凝视着你。月亮从天空中消失，你发现自己再次置身于聚集的风暴之下。\n\n"为什么——你为什么——"无形女王正在消散。她的存在本就过于脆弱，无法承受“午夜来临之刻”那化作武器的时间洪流。\n\n当她抽搐挣扎时，言语之风暴神开始膨胀、凝聚力量。它似乎并未完全被毁灭，而现在，它察觉到了本该征服它的对手的虚弱。
The Queen's courtiers are exceedingly polite. Some are even reverential. \n\n"The quest for the Cup is the highest of callings," explains her seneschal earnestly, nibbling at a wedge of cheese. "We are always pleased to harbour another sworn knight in our household."\n\nAfter a moment's consideration, he rummages in his pockets and retrieves the small, dripping corpse of some unidentifiable creature. It looks like a cross between worm and crab. "Here," he says. "This is all I can give you for now. I hope it helps on your quest."\n\nYou thank him as fulsomely as you can, and try not to sound too taken aback.=女王的朝臣们极为有礼，有些人的神态近乎虔敬。\n\n"对圣杯的追寻是最高的使命，"她的总管一边啃着一块奶酪，一边郑重其事地解释道，"我们一向乐于接纳又一位宣誓的骑士加入我们的宫廷。"\n\n思索片刻后，他从口袋中翻出某种难以辨认的生物的小尸体——正滴着水，看起来像是蠕虫与螃蟹的混合体。"来，"他说，"我现在只能给你这个。希望它能在你的旅途中有所帮助。"\n\n你尽可能满怀感激地道谢，同时努力掩饰自己的震惊。
The Queen's hand flies to her throat when you kneel and make your pledge. She seems both surprised and pleased. \n\n"If you truly wish to demonstrate your loyalty to me, my knight, I have but one plea." The Rat Brigade are nostalgic today. Each rat is competing to tell the most gruesome story of their past exploits. The time they infiltrated Sweet Jane's banquet at Port Prosper. The Rat Brigade are playing cards. Cinders is cheating – by means of mirrors concealed in the sleeves of her uniform, a spring-loaded trap, and brazen card-counting. The Taciturn Inspector reluctantly decides that if you are hiding anything, nobody would blame him for missing it. That is as close to 'innocent' as his precious forms allow. He waves you off without a word.=沉默寡言的检查官勉强做出判断：如果你真的藏了什么，那也不能怪他没查出来。在他那一丝不苟的表格中，这已经算是“清白”了。他一言不发地挥手让你离开。
The Taciturn Inspector takes three passes through the engine before reluctantly deciding that you probably, in all likelihood, have nothing to hide. He waves you on without a word.=沉默寡言的检查官在机车上来回巡视了三遍，最后不情不愿地承认你大概真的没藏什么。他一言不发地挥手让你离开。

The Titania Art Museum is already building a name for itself as one of Albion's greatest repositories of art, both real and modern. Any citizen can submit creations, and most of them have. Great halls are filled with statues in every material available, barely a wall lacks for a canvas whose paint has yet to completely dry.=狄坦妮娅艺术博物馆正逐步赢得“阿尔比恩最伟大艺术收藏地之一”的美誉，馆中收藏既有经典杰作，也有当代艺术品。任何市民皆可提交创作，而且大多数人都这么做了。巨大的展厅中陈列着各种材质的雕像，几乎每面墙上都挂着颜料尚未干透的画作。
The Titania Art Museum is open.=狄坦妮娅艺术博物馆现已开放。
The Titania Imperial Museum is open.=狄坦妮娅帝国博物馆现已开放。
The Titania Imperial Museum stands as a celebration of all nations of Earth, but mostly Britain. It features exhibits ranging from the Empress' 'liberation of India from the state of non-British rule' to the 'shameful split of the Traitor Colonies' in the 18th Century.\n\nA few quickly shushed voices ponder whether there might be a degree of colonial bias to some of these exhibits. These whisperers are directed to the Curator, a proud royalist happy to ease their concerns by informing them that no, there is not.=狄坦妮娅帝国博物馆是一场对地球各国（尤其是英国）的庆典。展览包括皇帝陛下“将印度从非英属状态中解放出来”的伟业，以及十八世纪“叛徒殖民地的可耻分裂”。\n\n少数声音悄声讨论，展览中是否存在某种殖民偏见。这些窃窃私语者被引向馆长——一位自豪的皇室主义者，他欣然安抚他们的疑虑，并告知：没有偏见，完全没有。
The Titanians are merry today, and already drunk - but they are not careless. Though they swirl their goblets, they do not spill their cordial. Much of it, at least.=狄坦妮娅人今天兴高采烈，而且已经喝醉了——但他们并不粗心大意。尽管他们旋转着手中的酒杯，但他们并没有洒出太多美酒。至少是大部分没有洒出。
The Titanians attend the ceremony, cheerfully eating grilled balls of pink plum-flavoured rice. You are joined on stage by the Formidable Lacquerer. She opens a cherry blossom fan, and cools herself.=狄坦妮娅人出席了仪式，兴高采烈地品尝着粉红色的梅子味烤饭团。令人敬畏的漆匠也加入了你的舞台行列。她打开了一把樱花扇，为自己扇风纳凉。
The Titanians chatter excitedly, as they stand in their elaborate silken robes, paper parasols held above their heads. Silence only falls over them as the Formidable Lacquerer shakes her bell-staff. =狄坦妮娅人身着精致的丝质长袍，头顶纸伞，兴奋地交谈着。只有当那位令人敬畏的漆匠摇动她的铃杆时，他们才安静下来。 
The Titanians giggle as a hummingbird perches on a crown of branches worn by a young woman. You're joined on stage by the Efflorescent Dandy  who sighs, wistfully.=当一只蜂鸟停歇在一位年轻女子头戴的树枝冠冕上时，狄坦妮娅人发出了咯咯的笑声。花枝招展的公子与你一同登台，他长叹一声。
The Titanians have built their pleasure district here. Scenes from rustic idylls play out around you: in the imported stretches of grass, and piles of hay, and beds of flowers.=狄坦妮娅人在此建造了他们的娱乐区。你周围上演着田园牧歌般的场景：在进口的草地上，成堆的干草旁，以及花丛之中。
The Titanians have built their pleasure district here. The artists here paint their faces in the most wonderful, extravagant ways.=狄坦妮娅人在此建造了他们的娱乐区。这里的艺术家们以最为奇妙、奢华的方式在脸上作画。
The Titanians have built their pleasure district here. The hieroglyphics are easier to interpret here than elsewhere.=狄坦妮娅人在此建造了他们的娱乐区。这里的象形文字比其他地方的更容易解读。
The Titanians have erected a May Pole deep within the Palace. They run their circles around it, winding it up with ribbons. A few of their number, standing by the pole, allow themselves to be wrapped.=狄坦妮娅人在宫殿深处竖起了一根五月柱。他们围着它转圈，用丝带将它缠绕起来。他们中的一些人，站在柱子旁边，任由自己被丝带缠绕。
The Titanians have stained their skin with petals, all the rainbow's colours on display through their diaphanous garments. The Efflorescent Dandy's corsage is solemnly bound with twine. They could all pass as members of a fae court, if they wished.=狄坦妮娅人用花瓣染红了皮肤，五彩斑斓的颜色透过他们透明的衣裳熠熠生辉。花枝招展的公子的胸花被庄重地用绳子系着。如果他们愿意，他们完全可以冒充精灵宫廷的成员。
The Titanians patrol the fortress in cloths and blue crowns. They practice the art of archery, aiming at faux-Correspondence sigils on posters scattered throughout the courtyard. The Pharaonic Enthusiast watches them solemnly from within the fortress, stroking her beard.=狄坦妮娅人身着衣物，头戴蓝色王冠，在要塞中巡逻。他们练习射箭的艺术，目标是散布在庭院各处的海报上伪造的通讯徽章。法老的爱好者在要塞内庄重地注视着他们，抚摸着她的胡须。
The Titanians patrol the fortress in fearsome masks and scales. They practice slicing a Chorister carapace as they draw their sword. The Formidable Lacquerer watches them intently from within the fortress, planning strategies for the next apocalypse.=狄坦妮娅人戴着令人畏惧的面具，身披鳞甲在要塞中巡逻。他们拔剑出鞘，练习切割唱诗蜂甲壳。令人敬畏的漆匠在要塞内密切关注着他们，为下一次大灾难策划战略。
The Titanians slip past you in a stream of silk. A young man, decoratively restrained with hempen rope trails after his compatriots.=狄坦妮娅人像丝绸般流畅地从你身边滑过。一个年轻人，被装饰性的麻绳轻轻束缚着，跟随着他的同胞们。
The Titanians stand gathered in the kaleidoscopic light of many paper lanterns, strung from strings overhead. You're joined on stage by the Formidable Lacquerer, who watches the lanterns serenely.=狄坦妮娅人聚集在多色彩纸灯笼所投射的斑斓光影之中，这些灯笼由头顶的细线串联。令人敬畏的漆匠与你一同登台，她宁静地注视着这些灯笼。
The Titanians struggle to adjust to the low tables, their knees bowing at awkward angles; but they do take quickly to the sake.=狄坦妮娅人努力适应着低矮的桌子，他们的膝盖以别扭的角度弯曲着；但他们很快就喜欢上了清酒。.
The Titanians watch from windows as the Chorister Hive wrecks their hard work. At this point it seems more an inconvenience than an apocalypse. It never seems to occur to anyone to just move away. Still, you know what to do by now.=坦妮娅人透过窗户注视着唱诗蜂群摧毁他们的劳动成果。如今这更像是个麻烦，而非世界的终结。没有人想过要干脆搬走。无论如何，你已经知道该怎么应对了。
The Titanians wear nothing but violent-coloured paints. They practice their defence with what nature has endowed them: their arms, their legs, their nails and teeth. The Efflorescent Dandy trains with them, looking for the most elegant way of tearing off a Chorister stinger.= 狄坦妮娅人仅涂抹着鲜艳色彩的颜料，他们利用大自然赋予的一切进行防御训练：他们的手臂、双腿、指甲和牙齿。花枝招展的公子与他们一同训练，寻找撕下唱诗蜂毒针最优雅的方式。
The Titanians, eyes daubed with kohl, gather in their numbers. You are joined on the stage by the Pharaonic Enthusiast, who adjusts the scarab in her hair.=狄坦妮娅人，眼线涂得漆黑，纷纷聚集而来。舞台上，你与法老的爱好者一同亮相，她正调整着自己头发上的圣甲虫饰品。
The Tournament nears its end. Only two knights remain in the running.=会武即将落幕。仅剩的两位骑士仍在角逐冠军。
The Twins sit across from each other with knowing smiles. "I think you know what comes next," one says to you. \n\n"Don't hold it against us. We mean well for ourselves, each other, the Circus. But it's what we must do."=双胞胎相视而坐，带着会心微笑。"你该知晓接下来会发生什么，"其中一人对你说。\n\n"别责怪我们。我们为彼此、为马戏团着想，但这是必经之路。"
The Unassailable Founder's bat-mask is impassive, her voice anything but. "The Berrenger is a haven for free-thinkers and freedom-fighters, for libertines and liberators and librarians," she says. "A lecture-hall for those who prefer unbounded darkness to a cage of light. And if you disagree, well - the Debating Halls are there, second door on your left."=无懈可击的创始人戴着蝙蝠面具，表情冷峻，语气却饱含热情。"贝伦格是一处庇护所，欢迎思想自由者与自由斗士，浪荡子、解放者与图书馆员。"她说，"这里是讲堂，属于那些宁愿沉浸于无边黑暗，也不愿困于明亮囚笼的人。若你对此有异议——辩论厅在那边，左手第二扇门。"

The Unreasonably Chirpy Administrator is used to flooding visitors with paperwork, not the other way around. The Loquacitor slides form after form across her desk - certificates of authenticity, notices of intent, requests, licenses, promissories. The Administrator waves you through from somewhere behind the document mountain.=那位异常快活的行政官惯于向访客狂轰滥炸各类文书，而不是反过来受到文书的轰炸。但话痨鬼将一张张表格推到她桌上——真伪证明、意向通知、申请书、许可证、承诺书。行政官从那座文书之山后面向你挥了挥手，示意你可以通过了。
The Unseen Queen has assigned you two tasks to prove you are worthy of the Martyr-King's Cup=无形女王给你分配了两项任务，它们将证明你有资格获得殉道王圣杯
The Unseen Queen has assigned you two tasks to prove you are worthy of the Martyr-King's Cup.=无形女王给了你两项任务，它们将证明你是否有资格获得殉道王圣杯。
The Unseen Queen has supplanted her father's place in the heavens. She wears his lingering torment as her mantle.=无形女王取代了她父亲在天穹的地位。她披戴着他挥之不去的折磨。
The Unseen Queen is ascending to the sky and donning the flayed ghost of her father's torment. A crown of lightning has begun to dance across her brow; a robe of roiling clouds is wrapping her in an embrace. With each passing second, she strengthens her claim on existence. Soon, Albion will have a moon again.=无形女王正在升向天空，披着她父亲受尽折磨后的剥皮幽魂。一顶雷霆王冠开始在她的额上跃动，一袭翻滚的云雾披风正将她环绕怀抱。每过一秒，她的存在都变得愈加坚定。不久，阿尔比恩将再度迎来它的月亮。
The Unseen Queen's castle is many leagues from your own, and you have ridden a great distance to get here. As you approach from a distance, you can see the castle sitting splendidly atop a mountain, silhouetted against the moon. Reaching it, however, is another matter entirely. \n\nThe path becomes too steep for your exhausted destrier; you will have to proceed on foot, if you can find a path at all through the thick gorse.=无形女王的城堡离你所在之地有数个国度之遥，你为此跋涉了漫长旅途。当你远远望去时，能看到城堡华丽地矗立在山巅，在月光下显出轮廓。\n\n但真正抵达那里，却远非易事。道路对你那疲惫不堪的战马来说已太过陡峭，你只能徒步前行——如果你能在这片荆棘丛生之地找出一条通路的话。
The Unseen Queen's portrait has been destroyed.=无形女王的画像已经被毁。
The Unseen Queen's throne-room is a ceaseless bustle of activity; servants attend her every whim, courtiers bombard her with anecdotes and wait in suspense for her laughter, stewards bring her petitions and scrolls. She waved them all away as soon as the herald announced your arrival, but they soon creep back around her.=无形女王的王座厅永远喧闹不休：仆人们服侍她的每一个心血来潮，朝臣们向她倾诉逸闻轶事，期待她的笑声，管家则带来请愿书和卷轴。传令官一宣布你的到来，她便挥手让他们全部退下——但不久之后，他们便悄然回到了她身边。

The Wood is vast and dark. Its silences are split by only the occasional downpour of rain from a passing stellar drift. Heavy firs bend in the wind. The Regent’s Grave broods over the forest from its high place.=这片林地广阔而幽暗。只有星际流云带来的阵雨偶尔打破其沉寂。沉重的杉树在风中摇曳。摄政王之墓在高处俯瞰整座森林，阴郁沉思。
The Workshop is open. Scientists gather here to develop new inventions and devices.=工坊已开放。科学家们聚集于此，研发各类新发明与装置。
The ages have not been kind.=岁月并未宽待于它。
The air hangs heavy with the ancient stench of blood, gristle and decaying flesh. The Hermit kneels by the still breathing body of some no longer identifiable animal, their sticky fingers questing for whatever wisdom may lie within flesh and bones.=空气中弥漫着血液、软骨和腐烂肉体的衰朽的恶臭，隐士跪坐在一些早已停止呼吸，躯体难以识别的动物旁，用他黏糊糊的手指在残存的骨骼和血肉中寻求任何可能的知识。
The air is humid and moist. The Agbonon Spring is being drained in preparation for the Season.=空气湿润而潮湿。为了迎接季节的到来，阿波农之泉正在排空。
The air is liquid. The floor is gas. The meat is tender.=空气如液体。地面如气体。你的肉体松软起来，如同被炖得极嫩。
The air is sickly sweet with decay.=空气中弥漫着腐败的腥甜。
The air is suddenly as hot and dry as the heart of a workworld. The incinerator has been switched on. Something is burning inside. Peering in, you can make out the remains of a bundle of black and silver uniforms, smouldering into ash.=空气骤然变得像工作世界的中心那样炙热干燥。焚化炉启动了，有什么东西正在燃烧。你探头望去，依稀可见一堆黑银制服的残骸正化为灰烬。

The assistant pressures you into ordering today's special: a slice of sesame cake layered with chocolate, and a masala chai. The former's superb, the latter's adequate: it stinks of kava and honeyed wasps. Either way, you regret nothing.=餐馆的助理强烈要求你点今日的特色菜肴：一份涂着巧克力的芝麻分层蛋糕和一杯马萨拉茶。前者是一流的，后者亦可圈可点尽管它散发着一股卡瓦和蜂蜜的臭味。无论怎样，你并不后悔。
The astronomers are proud to present you a slim volume. "It's a meditation, funnily enough, on prison escapes. Wardens becoming the jailed, and their usurpers writing the Laws. We're not sure what it means either."=天文学家们很自豪地向您介绍一本薄薄的书。 "这是一本沉思录，有趣的是，关于越狱。典狱长变成了被监禁者，而他们的篡位者写下了法律。我们也不确定它是什么意思。"
The astronomers, however, have nothing to hand you. They are bruised; their knuckles are bloodied. "We had an academic disagreement," one says. "Let it suffice to say we fell in love and that bred hate. We're better now that we've stopped looking at the star, however."=然而，天文学家们没有什么可以交给你。他们伤痕累累；他们的指关节血迹斑斑。"我们有一个学术分歧，"一个人说。"我想说的是，我们相爱了，却产生了仇恨。不过，现在我们不再看那颗星了，我们会好一些。"
The attendant finishes applying the brush to her wig. "I don't suppose you're familiar with Her Majesty personally? No?" She quirks an eyebrow in her mirror. "Ah well. I don't suppose tonight's client is either. How amusing."=侍者给她的假发上完妆。"你不会恰好认识女王陛下吧？不认识？"她对着镜子扬了扬眉。"嗯，那今晚的客人八成也不认识。真不错。"
The attendant sighs as you go over her lines together. "I don't even know what a baronet is." She frowns. "Some kind of root vegetable?"=你和侍者一起反复排练台词，她叹了口气。"我甚至不知道男爵是什么。"她皱着眉，"某种根茎类蔬菜？"

The barber smiles at you with too many teeth. "Shave and a nick?" he asks. Though his shop is lushly appointed, crushed velvet and a decadently papal aesthetic, it is conspicuously empty. He notes your glance and, crestfallen, offers to sell you a story instead.=理发师粗犷地笑着，露出一大嘴牙齿，问道 "理发剃须吗?" 尽管他的理发店装潢豪华，绒面丝绸与奢靡的教皇风相搭，却无人问津。他顺着你的目光看了一眼，神情瞬间暗淡，随后悻悻地提出，要不，花点钱听他讲个故事？
The barber smiles at you with too many teeth. "Shave and a nick?" he asks. Though his shop is lushly appointed, crushed velvet and a decadently papal aesthetic, it is conspicuously empty. The beast howls as the flames race across it, burning fast on subcutaneous layers of curdled fat and oil. Its long, bony fingers curl like the legs of a dying spider. The cafe throngs with well-coiffed geriatrics, their raiments quaintly anachronistic. You acquire a lemon meringue tart and some hibiscus tea, both fabulous, and retreat to a plush chair, where you spend the afternoon eavesdropping on a couple discussing their dead friends.=茶馆涌入了一群精心梳妆的老年人，他们的衣物有着一种早已过时的优雅。你点了一份美味的酸柠檬蛋白酥和木槿茶，倚靠在那张偷听一对夫妻讨论他们已逝故友来消磨下午时光的舒适的椅子上。
The camp is abuzz with activity. Errant young medievalists flood the grove, busying themselves erecting tents and arguing the finer points of the ablative case. The Dismal Paleographer looks almost happy, lecturing his new flock on the principles of Chivalry and Feudalism. The Vituperative Classicist glowers by the fire. The Feckless Theologian looks tremendously put out.=营地里一片喧嚣。稚气未脱的中世纪研究者挤满林间空地，忙着搭帐篷，并为夺格变化的细微差别争得面红耳赤。阴郁的古物学家看起来几乎称得上愉快，正向他的新门徒讲授骑士精神与封建主义的基本原则。挑剔的古典学者在火边怒目而视，软弱的神学家则显得极其不高兴。
The camp is alive with plainsong. Hymns sound through the incense-choked trees. Young theologians have converged on Traitor's Wood, debating the respective merits of Boethius and Aquinas. The Feckless Theologian is in the thick of the activity, providing wine and commentaries on the most daring heresies. The Vituperative Classicist and the Dismal Theologian sit at opposite ends of the camp, scowling at their gathered piles of evidence.=营地中响起平调圣歌。圣歌声穿过烟雾缭绕的树林。年轻神学家聚集于叛徒之林，争论波伊修斯与阿奎那的思想孰优孰劣。软弱的神学家活跃其间，为最具胆识的异端思想提供酒水与注解。挑剔的古典学者与阴郁的古物学家则分坐营地两端，阴沉地盯着自己那一堆堆的证据。
The camp is deserted. A cold wind blows the ashes off the campfire. A few torn pages from a monograph, remorselessly scribbled over, are torn away. A nightingale calls from somewhere in the dark wood. Rain is beginning to fall, washing away the detritus left behind.=营地空无一人。一阵冷风将篝火上的灰烬吹散。几页潦草涂写的专著残页被风卷起。林中夜莺啼鸣。雨开始落下，冲刷着留下的残骸。
The camp is deserted. The scholars have left the wood: there is nothing left for them here. The banners of Summerset flap forlornly in the wind.=营地已被废弃。学者们已离开这片林地：这里再无可供他们研究之物。夏暮的旗帜在风中孤零地飘扬。
The candles sputter and extinguish. The knights freeze in place, still as statues. \n\nA pit opens beneath the Blindness Throne and swallows you whole. The captain describes a field of debris they flew through to reach the signal box. Each rock was bursting with life. Tiny flowers the colour of milk jostled in the currents of the engine. The locomotive was overwhelmed with the scent of honey, and home.=车长记述了他们在抵达信号箱途中穿越的一片碎石带。那里的每一块岩石都充满了生命。乳白色的小花在机车的气流中摇曳。整辆列车弥漫着蜂蜜与家的香气。
The captain describes the dapple of distant starlight falling over their engine; the long hours spent in the watchful dark, suddenly shattered by a dazzle of cold radiance. When will they know such splendour again?=车长描述着远方星光洒落在机车上的斑驳光影。他们在警觉的黑暗中度过漫长时光，直至被一道冷峻的光辉所打破。他们何时才能再见如此光芒？
The captain details various damages done to their engine, and the causes. Hull: aggrieved cantankeri. Backplate: bad launch off Port Prosper. Frontplate: bad landing on Port Prosper. Hull again – Jones. A change in personnel gives the captain optimism for the next leg of the journey.=车长详细记录了机车所遭受的各类损伤及其原因。车体：受到愤怒的顽固虱撞击；后板：从兴荣港起飞时撞歪；前板：在兴荣港降落时撞歪；再一次，车体 —— 是琼斯的责任。人员调动使得车长对接下来的旅程抱有希望。
The captain warns of a condition afflicting her crew: it started during a period of downtime. One took up gazing at the stars, and encouraged the others into consideration of the heavens. This has since become a fixation, and the entire crew only grudgingly leaves the windows to perform their labours.=车长警告称，她的列车员受到了某种影响：最初是在一次休整期间开始的。有一人开始凝视星空，并鼓动他人也开始面对苍穹沉思。自那时起，这种行为便变得根深蒂固，全体列车员如今都只在窗前凝视的空隙勉强前去履行职务。
The captain writes feverishly of a light that danced at some distance before them. After some hours following it, it abruptly grew, getting brighter as it did. Then nothing, only the sense of something lost without a chance to understand.=车长狂热地写下那团在远处跳动的光。跟随它数小时后，它突然变大，愈发明亮。然后便什么都没有了，只剩下某种失落之感，来不及理解便已错过。
The captain writes feverishly of a light that danced at some distance before them. After some hours following it, it abruptly grew, getting brighter as it did. Then nothing. Just— Gone.=车长狂热地写下那团在远处跳动的光。跟随它数小时后，它突然变大，愈发明亮。然后——什么都没有了。就这样……消失了。
The captain writes of a homestead their locomotive passed when journeying from Port Avon. The buildings were in the style of old London. The nostalgia brought the crew together, after a near disastrous encounter with a Chorister bee.=车长写道，他们的列车从埃文村出发，途中曾经过一处宅地。那些房屋是旧伦敦的风格。这份怀旧之情使列车员们重新团结在一起 —— 此前他们才与遭遇一只唱诗蜂，几乎酿成灾难。
The captain writes of a vast object that drew a burning arc across their path. It seared a line into their vision and the navigator's heart. She insisted on trying to follow it for days. =车长写下某个巨大物体划出一条燃烧轨迹，横越他们的航线。它在他们的视野中灼出一道光痕，也灼入领航员的心中。她执意要追随那物体数日。

The clerk leads you through an interminable series of hallways, through offices, studies, and libraries. At last, you arrive at a spiral staircase of wrought iron. You descend flight after flight, the Brigade growing heavy on your shoulders. Eventually, you emerge into a chamber whose walls are set with many metal doors. The clerk unlocks one and steps aside.=文员带你穿过一条条长到没边的走廊，穿过一间间办公室、书房与图书馆。终于，你们来到一座锻铁螺旋楼梯前。你一层层向下走，感觉肩上的老鼠大队变得越来越沉。最终，你来到一间四壁嵌满金属门的房间。文员把其中一扇门打开，侧身让你进去。

The clock is fast. Sabotage. Someone has wound it ahead. The culprit soon reveals himself as an exhausted miner. "Please, I've done two shifts already," he whispers. "I got to sleep sometime, right?"=时钟被调快了。人为破坏。有人在故意把它往前拨。罪魁祸首很快败露——是一名筋疲力尽的矿工。"求你了，我已经连上了两班，"他虚弱地说，"总得让我睡会儿吧，对吗？"
The clock is fixed, though none pay it much attention. The clouds of hookah smoke emerging from the quieter rooms do more to toy with the passing of time than a crate of fresh Hours, never mind a little tinkering around with something as banal as a clock.=时钟被修复了，尽管无人会对此多加留意。 从静谧房间中飘出的水烟雾气，比一箱新挖出的时间矿更能戏弄时间的流逝，更不用说在一座普通时钟上那点微不足道的修修补补了。
The clock is next to the courthouse, where a Murderous Sort is in the dock before Judge 'Hang All The Filthy _______' Stacks. They have blood on their clothes and no alibi.=时钟建在一所法院的旁边，在那里一个看上去像是杀人犯的人正站在被告席上，面对着那位号称‘绞死所有肮脏的______’的斯塔克斯法官的审判。那人的衣服上沾了血，并且没有不在场证明。
The clock is running fast. One of the clients spots you looking at it and grabs your arm. "No," she begs, before being dragged away by a pair of expressionless nurses. "No more, please!"=时钟走快了。一名顾客注意到你在检查它，立刻抓住你的手臂。"别，" 她哀求道，随后便被两名面无表情的护士拖走，"求你了，别再来了！"

The clocks are running fast. Fixing them would be a several hour long boon for anyone in the more pleasant treatments.=时钟走快了。修正它们会给接受较愉悦治疗手段的病人带来几小时的额外享受。
The clocks are running several hours slow, much to the relief of a fellow Captain with a shipment of Hours from Lustrum. "Thought I was going to be late again," he tells you, quickly unloading his cargo.=一位从伍载镇运送时间矿的车长对着慢了几个小时的时钟松了口气。"还以为又要迟到了，"他对你说道，同时迅速地卸下车上货物。
The clocks are running slow here. A whole day, by your quick calculation. Still, it should be a small inconvenience for most.=这里的时钟走慢了。根据你的快速计算，慢了有整整一天的时间。不过，对大多数人来说，这应该只是个小小的不便。
The clocks are running slow. "Please, not yet," whispers a nervous tenant. "I still have to pay this month's rent! My landlord..."=时钟走慢了。"请别现在就修好它，"一位紧张的租户低声说道，"我还没交这个月的房租！我的房东……"
The clocks here all run slightly fast. Clients rarely notice, but over the course of a day it works out to an extra hour's break for their attendants.=这里的时钟全都略微快了一些。顾客们很少察觉，但一天下来，这些微小的时间偏差最终为侍者们争取到额外一小时的休息时间。
The clocks in London's Embassy are running slow, though it matters little to most of the port. The Eagle has no interest in Albion Standard Time, but it's playing merry hell with the London Ambassador's timekeeping.=伦敦大使馆的时钟慢了，不过对港口的大多数人来说，这无关紧要。雄鹰汗对阿尔比恩标准时间毫无兴趣，但这却彻底搅乱了伦敦大使的时间安排。
The clocks on the pier are running a few hours slow. "Explore the mists! Trip of a lifetime!" calls a diving bell attendant, one eye fixed on the slow tick of the minutes hand.=码头的时钟慢了几个小时。"探寻迷雾！一生难忘的旅程！"一名潜水钟的工作人员喊道，一只眼睛紧盯着缓慢移动的分针。
The cold crawls under blankets and into sky-suits. It splits pipes, freezes hatches, kills slowly and patiently.\n\n[This is your last chance.]=寒意爬进毯下、潜入潜天装中。它冻裂管道，封死舱口，缓慢而耐心地夺走一个个生命。\n\n[这是你最后的机会。]
The cold has come inside now. It hangs heavy in the corridors. It closes the pipes with killing ice. It gathers, like a stone, in your lungs.\n\n[You have two chances to find a lifeline before the cold claims you.]=寒冷已渗入车内，在走廊中沉重地弥漫。它用致命的寒冰封闭了管道。它像石头般聚集在你的肺中。\n\n[在寒冷将你吞没之前，你还有两次找到生机的机会。]

The first lecture you stumble upon has attracted a vast crowd, and you must fight to even get to see the speaker. He has a dejected posture, his voice is quiet but insistent. "Who made the Halved forgo its obligations?" he asks. "Who was the storyteller whose words extinguished a star?"=你偶然走近的第一个讲座吸引了一大群听众，你甚至得挤进去才能看到演讲者。他姿态黯然，声音虽轻，却坚定不移。"是谁让那位“半分者”放弃了自己的义务？"他问，"谁是那位用言语熄灭了群星的说书人？"
The flora of the Leadbeater & Stainrod Nature Reserve loom taller than its high iron walls. The researchers on the walls do not notice you.=李德彼特&斯坦罗德的植物群高过铁墙。墙上的研究者们无人注意到你们。
The footman disappears into the gloom, leaving you alone facing a desk shaped like a baptismal font. A Cheery Registrar sits in the middle, waiting to greet you.=男仆消失在黑暗中，只留下你一个人面对着一张形似洗礼池的桌子。一位热情的登记员坐在中间，等待迎接你。
The frames shop, as it turns out, is not where you purchase corrective glasses. Instead, it hawks poor metaphors and second-rate similes, phrases with which to inadequately frame a situation. Not wanting to be impolite, you buy an analogy for an upset stomach and ignore the way they harvest fresh stock from a gaggle of weeping men.=所谓的“画框店”，其实并不售卖矫正眼镜，兜售的是蹩脚的隐喻和拙劣的比喻，勉强能用来“勾勒”某种境况。为了不显得失礼，你买下了一个形容胃部不适的比喻，并对店员从一群哭泣的男人身上采集新商品的行为视而不见。
The frames shop, as it turns out, is not where you purchase corrective glasses. Instead, it hawks poor metaphors and second-rate similes, phrases with which to inadequately frame a situation. Not wanting to be impolite, you buy an analogy for an upset stomach. =所谓的“画框店”，其实并不售卖矫正眼镜，兜售的是蹩脚的隐喻和拙劣的比喻，勉强能用来“勾勒”某种境况。为了不显得失礼，你买下了一个形容胃部不适的比喻。 
The front of the engine is even more infected with sprouting mushrooms and sporing fungal growths. The engine room lies ahead, seemingly the source of the persistent movement. Before that, however, is a haze of spores rendering the corridor ahead entirely noxious.=机车前部被发芽的蘑菇和孢子状的真菌感染得更为严重。引擎室在前面，似乎是持续运动的源头。然而，在到达那里之前，一片孢子的阴霾使前面的走廊变得毒气充溢。
The gathered Titanians resemble a pleasant meadow; though instead of wildflowers blooming from blades of grass, they peek out from strands of hair. You are joined on stage by the Efflorescent Dandy, who is quickly finishing an apple.=聚集的狄坦妮娅人宛如一片宜人的草地；尽管不是野花从草叶间绽放，而是从他们的发丝间探出头来。舞台上，你与正在迅速吃完一个苹果的花枝招展的公子一同亮相。
The gland your crew found was dried up and almost worthless. But the Curator does not seem without hope. Speak to it aboard your engine.=列车员发现的腺体已经干枯无用。但是负责人似乎还没有绝望，上机车和它谈谈
The grand chamber of the Morning Room is overseen by a Dowager Duchess fairly dripping with emeralds. You're amazed she can lift her wrists.=晨间厅这座华丽殿堂由一位戴满祖母绿的老年女公爵主持。你简直无法想象她如何抬得起双腕。
The graveyard is at the end of this quiet street. Not many people travel here; the electric lights are fewer, barely keeping away the dark.=墓地坐落于寂静街道尽头。鲜有人迹至此；电灯稀疏寥落，勉强抵御着黑暗侵袭。
The grinding and guttering of the looms Below Stairs adds an additional layer of horror to an already terrifying procession.=楼下织机的轧响与嗤咯声，为这本就恐怖的行进增添了一层新的惊惧。
The ground gives way to a bog – grey and fetid as a newly slain corpse. It burps lazily. The marsh reeds susurrate, growing in volume as you approach. There is English amidst the polyphony: names – yours and those of your crew.=地面塌陷成一片沼泽——灰色而腐臭，如同一具刚死的尸体。它懒洋洋地冒着泡。沼泽的芦苇发出沙沙声，随着你的靠近，声浪越来越大。那混杂的低语中竟夹杂着英语：是人名——你的名字，还有你列车员们的名字。
The gruff gentleman with the complexion of a boiled ham frowns through the steam. He bellows an enquiry as to your health. He is here to ease the excruciations inflicted by his joints. The words he uses to describe the climb to the pool are rarely heard out of engine boiler rooms. =那位皮肤红润、神情严肃的老绅士隔着蒸汽向你皱眉。他高声问候你的健康。他来此是为缓解折磨他的关节疼痛。他形容上山途中之艰难所用的词语，往往只在引擎炉房中听到。
The handful of shades on your crew cluster together at your end of the table, pulling their masks fractionally forwards to admit their forks. You are careful not to catch a glimpse of what lies beneath. Your living crew sit at the other end of the table, talking quietly among themselves.=你列车员中为数不多的几位遗魂聚在餐桌一端，稍稍将面具向前拉开，以便将叉子送入口中。你小心不去窥视面具下隐藏的东西。活着的列车员们坐在桌子的另一端，低声交谈。
The hard voice of a drill sergeant, dispensing the day's punishment. How you hated him.\n=训练教官分配当天的惩罚时的严厉声音。你真讨厌他。
The homes are equally lovely. They could have been plucked from some far-away idyll on Earth, rather than here in the High Wilderness.=这些房屋同样可爱。它们仿佛是从地球上某个如牧歌般的远方土地摘取而来，而非来自这天野。
The hour hands on the clock in your cabin spin round and round again. Something chimes.=你舱室内的时针不停旋转，一圈又一圈。钟声响起。
The hour-harker looks embarrassed about bringing you here. Not enough to return your Sovereigns, of course.=时间探子带你来这里时显得有些尴尬——当然，这点尴尬并不足以让他退还你的金币。
The hovering instant between opening your mouth and saying something regrettable.=张口与说出令人遗憾话语间的徘徊瞬间。
The insistent cry of a nuthatch echoes through the wood=一只䴓鸟执着的啼鸣在树林中回荡
The insistent cry of a nuthatch echoes through the wood. =一只䴓鸟执着的啼鸣在树林中回荡。
The insistent shriek of a whistle. You find the whistle, abandoned, on the galley floor.=一声尖锐刺耳的哨声响起。你在厨房地板上发现了那只被人遗弃的哨子。
The journey is disturbed. As you traverse the relay-passage, some celestial beast thunders amidst the roots of the sky. Its lamentable bellows shake the heavens.=旅途受到扰动。当你穿越中继通道时，某只天界巨兽在天空的根系中雷鸣般穿行。它哀恸的咆哮震动苍穹。
The keening of the hours as they are spun backwards makes your legs weak and your heart pound. You feel unaccountably wearied.=时间被倒纺的哀鸣让你双腿发软、心跳加速。你感到莫名的疲惫。
The kettle is on, filling the room with steam. Have you seen this room before? Her face is behind a veil, but the voice is right, as perfect as you've heard in dreams. "I've sent everyone away so we can talk properly. You still take sugar, yes?" =水壶在炉上沸腾，蒸汽弥漫了整个房间。你曾经来过这里吗？她的脸藏在面纱后，但声音一如你梦中听到的那般完美无瑕。"我已经让其他人离开了，这样我们才能好好谈谈。你还是喜欢加糖，对吧？"
The land here is so damp, anything built on it likely sank into the marsh many years ago.=这片土地潮湿至此，凡是建在这里的东西，多半早在多年前便沉入了沼泽。
The lanes bustle with visitors. The widow Campbell's guest house is turning people away. The Nowhere inn glows with bright lamps, and thumps with the sound of music-hall songs.=巷子里的游客熙熙攘攘。寡妇坎贝尔的客房已经住满。无处旅馆里灯火通明，内部音乐厅的歌声节奏顿挫。
The largest chunk of the Cantankeri still lives. They can be slow to die. Stubbornness, perhaps, or the sluggish journey of signals along their calcified nerves.=顽固虱最大的那块碎片还活着。这种生物的死亡是一个缓慢的过程。或许是因为固执，或许是因为神经信号在钙化的神经中传递的比较慢。
The last of your fuel is gone. The fires of your engine dim and die. Your funnel coughs its last.\n\nAlready, the cold of the High Wilderness is crawling frosty fingers across your windows. Your remaining hopes are all desperate.\n\n[You have three chances to find a lifeline before the cold claims you.]=你最后的燃料耗尽了。机车的火焰渐弱、熄灭。烟囱咳出了最后一口气。\n\n此刻，天野严寒那冰霜的手指已缓缓爬上你的车窗。你仅存的希望都近乎绝望。\n\n[在寒冷将你吞没之前，你还有三次找到生机的机会。]
The leader of your former cell is waiting on the bed in the room, his dark hair bound up in a bandana. The years have left him more scarred, but he still carries his hookah pipe and his little black book. "Suppose I could finally tell you a few names." He gives you a crooked smile. "Or maybe you're here for something a little more friendly like?"=从前的牢房里，你的领导人正在房间的床上等待，他的黑发用头巾束起。岁月给他留下了更多的伤痕，但他仍然带着他的烟斗和他的小黑书。"我终于可以告诉你几个名字了。"他给了你一个狡黠的微笑。"或者，也许你来这里是为了一些更友好的事情？"
The light fails. The roses sicken, wither, and are gone.=光芒消逝了。玫瑰们生病，枯萎，凋零了。
The light is a flushed yellow, autumnally ripe.=光线泛着红润的黄色，宛如秋日的成熟果实。
The light is thin, winter-bright.=光线微弱，色如冬日。
The light is warm, a reminder of summer.=光线温暖，令人想起盛夏时节。
The light's the colour of a good dessert wine, uncorked in autumn.=光线的色泽如一瓶上好的秋酿甜酒。
The lights of the port slowly fade. Beyond lies a forested grey swamp, misted over and filled with leafless black trees that claw at the air.=港口的灯光渐渐远去。前方是一片灰色的森林沼泽，迷雾笼罩，枯黑的树木仿佛利爪般撕扯着空气。
The longer you spend in Worlebury-juxta-Mare, the easier it is to spot the flaws in the facade. There! Another detail, not quite right.=你在滨海沃伯瑞呆的时间越长，就越容易发现其表面的缺陷。在那里！另一个不大对劲的细节。
The maggots under your skin are quiet, for now.=你皮下的蛆虫暂时安静了下来。
The magician could be at Port Prosper.=魔术师可能在兴荣港。
The magician could be at Titania=魔术师可能在狄坦妮娅
The magician could be at Titania.=魔术师可能在狄坦妮娅。
The magician could be in Lustrum=魔术师可能在伍载镇
The magician could be in Lustrum.=魔术师可能在伍载镇。
The marsh seems to go on forever. Increasingly it feels as if it actually might.=沼泽仿佛永无尽头。这种感觉正变得越来越真实。
The marsh-gas shimmers in front of your eyes. It stings. They bleed tears.=沼气在你眼前闪烁。它刺痛着你。你的眼眶中流出血泪。
The masked dead now comprise so many of your number that some of the living crew have begun wearing masks and swearing vows of silence to fit in. It makes for a solemn, gloomy meal, the only sound the clink of cutlery on bowls and on porcelain masks.=亡者的数量如今已多到使部分活着的列车员也开始戴上面具，并发誓沉默，以融入他们之中。晚餐气氛肃穆而沉郁，唯一的声音来自刀叉碰撞碗碟与瓷面具的清响。
The masked dead sit at one end of the table, the living at the other. The dead lift their masks forward to spoon food to their cold lips. The living crew are careful not to look. It's considered bad luck to glimpse the faces of the dead. You suppose new superstitions bloom quickly under these conditions.=亡者戴着面具，坐在餐桌一端；生者则坐在另一端。亡者将面具稍稍掀起，用勺将食物送至冰冷的唇边。活着的列车员刻意避免目光接触。看到亡者的脸被认为是不祥之兆。在这样的环境下，新迷信总是迅速滋生。
The melee is a terrifying thing, like a monster in itself. A heaving mass of thrashing, convulsing armour. Occasionally a battered knight will crawl out from among the stamping boots and collapse, exhausted, at the edge.=这场混战如同一头怪物般令人惊惧。它是一团翻腾的、抽搐的铠甲之海。时不时有一名被打得遍体鳞伤的骑士从乱脚中爬出，筋疲力尽地倒在场外。
The memory of another world begins to fade. Albion. London. The Reach. The stuff of dream. A childish fantasy.=另一个世界的记忆正逐渐淡去。阿尔比恩。伦敦。外野。梦之物语。孩童的幻想。
The mess has been converted into a nursery. Your Chief Engineer seems content here, which is a small mercy.=食堂已成了一个托儿所。你的轮机长似乎对当前的处境感到满意，这算是一种小小的慰藉。
The mid-morning calm is shattered by furious yelling in the distance. "The Psalmists are causing trouble again!" shouts a passing urchin joyously.=清晨的宁静被远处暴怒的叫喊打破。"诗篇教派又在惹事啦！"一个路过的顽童高兴地喊道。
The mist thickens. Shadows move in the dark.=雾气加重。黑暗中，有影子在移动。
The mists tickle. You paddle listlessly through their depths, breathing in the candy-sweet. This is really quite pleasant. =雾气拂过你的身子。你无精打采地划过雾的深处，呼吸着那糖果般的甜香。这感觉……相当惬意。
The mixture is about to set.=混合物即将凝固。
The mixture is becoming harder to stir.=混合物逐渐变得难以搅拌。
The mixture is hardening quickly.=混合物正在快速硬化。
The mixture is liquid and runny.=混合物呈液态且流动性好。
The mixture will not take any more additions.=混合物无法再添加任何物质。
The moonlight fades, and you find yourself back in the vault. \n\nThe swirling pit at the vault's centre has vanished; and when you examine the portrait of the Unseen Queen, you realise she is now holding a stone cup in her hands. Her face is mostly scratched out still, but you think you can perceive a smile.\n\nBefore you can question her, a distant bell begins tolling rather urgently.=月光渐渐褪去，你发现自己又回到了宝库之中。\n\n宝库中央的漩涡已然消失。当你细看无形女王的肖像时，你意识到她的双手如今正捧着一只石制圣杯。她的脸依旧被严重划损，但你似乎能察觉到一丝微笑。\n\n在你来得及质问她之前，一阵急促的钟声从远处响起。
The mountain giveth...=山的贡物…
The mountain sings no more.=大山不再歌唱。
The mud bubbles. The air here is poison.=泥潭沸腾，空气中满是毒气。
The mud clings to you. It burns slightly. Itches.=泥巴紧贴着你的皮肤，轻微灼痛，令人发痒。
The mud is hard here, covered with soft snow. A few young trees flourish in the inhospitable conditions, but not many. Only one other mining group seems to have staked a claim here. The hour-harker warns that it would be most impolite to ask about their success.=此地泥土坚硬，被一层柔软的积雪覆盖。一些幼树在恶劣环境中勉强生长，但数量不多。似乎只有另一组矿工在此认领了一块地。时间探子提醒道，询问他们是否成功是非常不礼貌的行为。
The mud is soft here, and the ground fertile. Though outside the boundaries of your prospective claim, giant trees spear through the snow and rise high over your head. It is close enough to town for off-duty miners to chop them down for firewood, much to the cheer of the chilly miners in Lustrum town.=这里泥土松软，土地肥沃。虽然这些巨树并不在你预定认领的范围内，但它们仍矗立在积雪中，高耸入云。此地离镇子不远，下班的矿工会来砍伐这些树作为柴火，这让伍载镇中受冻的矿工们颇为高兴。
The mud is soft here, under thick snow. There is no vegetation to be seen. Many claims have been staked here and their owners continue to work them.=雪下泥地松软，四周寸草不生。已有许多认领地设于此地，其主人们仍在继续挖掘。
The mud under your feet bubbles furiously. A fetid smell of dead flesh rises up.=你脚下的泥地剧烈地冒着泡。一股腐肉的恶臭扑鼻而来。
The names of all your old crew, and what became of them.=你以前所有船员的名字，以及他们后来的情况。
The names of every person you censored, and the reasons they required censure.=你审查过的每一个人名，以及他们为何被审查。
The names of every work you censored, and the reasons they required censure.=你审查过的每一部作品，以及它们为何需要被审查。
The names of your professors at the University.=你在大学时期那些教授的名字，一一浮现。
The new Governor is meeting with representatives of London=新任总督正在与伦敦的代表会面
The new Governor is meeting with representatives of London.=新任总督正在会见来自伦敦的代表。
The new relay station is now functioning.=新中转站现已投入运行。
The note says: 'Lustrum? And what about the Logoi? Or – "Swallowed by the past in a place where the weft of time had frayed" – What does this mean?'=纸条上写着：‘伍载镇里？那圣言里面呢？或者 "于时间纬线的磨损处被过去吞噬" ，这句话什么意思？
The officials of the Bureau of Entertainments are teeth and the gleam of brass buttons, no warmth at all. They do not trust you.=娱乐事务局的官员们如同锋利的牙齿与闪亮的黄铜纽扣一般，没有丝毫温度。他们不信任你。
The officials of the Bureau of Entertainments round up the crowd and lead them outside in a single file, their grins unchanging as the governments of death.=娱乐局的官员将人群赶成一列，领他们鱼贯而出，官员脸上的笑容毫无改变，一如死亡的统治一般。
The only way forward is to forge through a bitterly cold river. Tiny creatures bite at your flesh the whole way.=你不得不涉过一条刺骨冰冷的河流，无数微小的生物一路啃咬你的皮肤。
The opinion columnist, who has notorious Tackety sympathies, makes acid-tongued references to 'the lapdog captains of London,' of which you presume you are one.=那位同情钉鞋党的意见专栏作家对'伦敦的哈巴狗队长们'发表了尖酸刻薄的言论，你觉得你自己会是其中之一。
The other lecture to catch your attention is being boomed across the Forum by a tall, grotesquely muscular man with a beard that explodes from beneath his mask. His voice is both extremely loud and mesmerizing in its sing-song cadence.=另一个吸引你注意的讲座正回荡在整个广场上，由一位高大、肌肉夸张的男子主讲。他面具下蓬勃生长的胡须仿佛要炸裂开来。他的声音既极其洪亮，又带着催眠般的吟唱节奏。
The other lecture to entice you is that given by a diminutive old woman, speaking in a dry whisper to only three Aspirants. But you catch the phrase "coils of shattering ice, a thousand feet high."=另一个吸引你注意的讲座是由一位身材矮小的老妇人主持的，她用干涩的耳语对仅有的三名有志者低声细语地讲着什么。但你听见了这样一句话："碎裂冰蛇，千尺高。"
The other lecture you come across is being delivered by a lithe, confident figure of indeterminate gender. They are expounding on their topic at such length that you have trouble deciphering what they're talking about. Finally, you identify the topic: a certain Mr Barleycorn.=你遇到的另一个讲座由一位身形灵巧、自信满满、性别难辨的人主讲。此人长篇大论地讲述着某个主题，让你一时间难以理解其意。最终，你才辨认出主题：麦寸先生。
The other lecture you notice is being delivered by a compact woman of unmistakably military bearing, with one leg that appears to have been replaced below the knee by a fire-poker. She is talking about something called 'the Binary.' A team of Recalcitrants are trying, but failing, to shout her down.=另一个被你注意到的讲座是由一位体格紧凑、具有明显军人气质的女子主持的。她的一条腿似乎在膝盖以下被一根火钩取而代之。她正在讲述某种被称为‘双生者’的存在。一群顽抗者正试图打断她，但显然未能成功。
The other lecture you stumble upon has attracted a vast crowd, and you must fight to even get see the speaker. He has a dejected posture, his voice is quiet but insistent. "Who made the Halved forgo its obligations?" he asks. "Who was the storyteller whose words extinguished a star?"=你偶然走近的另一个讲座吸引了一大群听众，你甚至得挤进去才能看到演讲者。他姿态黯然，声音虽轻，却坚定不移。"是谁让那位“半分者”放弃了自己的义务？"他问，"谁是那位用言语熄灭了群星的说书人？"
The other masked figures leave you a wide berth as you make your way down. When you turn to them, they flinch away, as though expecting you to launch into invective. =当你往下走时，其他戴着面具的人都离你远远的。当你面对他们时，他们会退缩，好像觉得你马上就要开始谩骂一样。

"I look forward to working for you," they say in a monotone voice.=灵魂轻抚你同伴的面庞，情绪激动得颤抖不已。乳白色的泪水自它面颊滑落，流入同伴的口中。\n\n同伴那皮革般的嘴唇微微颤动，转头望向你，完全无视了灵魂。起初动作僵硬，但渐渐变得流畅，它坐直身子，从基座上站下，落在炉室的地板上。"我期待为您效力，"它用毫无感情的声音说道。
The spirit under trial is a man who dabbled with immortality when he was alive. He died in the end, but he didn't quite die all the way.=受审的鬼魂是一位生前曾涉足不死之术的男子。他终究死去了，但也没有完全死透。
The spirit under trial is a woman who committed horrific crimes in life. The Jewelled Judge is sparing with the details, but the crimes seem to be particularly ambitious, mass-scale atrocities.=受审的鬼魂是一位生前犯下骇人罪行的女子。珠玉法官对具体细节讳莫如深，但她所犯的似乎是极具野心的大规模暴行。
The spirit under trial is an Ephemera who has been bounced around the Blue Kingdom for decades. Its case-file is a stack of stone tablets that the Jewelled Judge reads through with evident horror. The spirit just wants to be declared Dead, but its papers have been repeatedly misfiled, its soul raises all sorts of false alarms among the spirit-measurers, and its corpse was exhumed and used in unsettling experiments. All this has apparently greatly complicated its post-mortem status.=受审的鬼魂是一位在蓝色王国中辗转多年的浮生灵。它的案卷是一大摞石板，珠玉法官在审阅时流露出惊恐之色。这鬼魂只想被宣判为‘已死’，但它的档案屡次被归错、灵体又不断触发鬼魂测量仪的误报，它的尸体还曾被挖出，在上面进行过令人不安的实验。这一切将它的死后状态变作了一团糨糊。
The spirit's calcification has progressed too far, now. Its salt-flesh crumbles as you watch.=遗魂的钙化过程已经无法逆转。在你的目光下，它的盐质身躯开始崩碎。

The stoker advances, but there is a puff of smoke, and the Illusive Forged Companion disappears. "I am more difficult to replace," the Companion says in response to your expression.=司炉奋力尝试终结这位锻造同伴的生命，但拳头落在毫无感情的金属之上。当他停下喘息的一刻，同伴一拳击中他的胸膛。此人顿时瘫倒在地，一命呜呼。"我比他更难以替代，"同伴看着你脸上的神情，如此回应。

The tale has grown in the telling, and grown, and grown. In its vastness, it has become impossible to control. You find yourself searching through previous Cantos to ensure consistency of character and detail – stumbling unexpectedly upon narrative dead ends – writing yourself into terrible tangles that cannot be salvaged. With the grim determination of Hercules facing the hydra, you cut. And cut. Until the story lies bleeding, but better, at your feet. The Fifth Canto is complete.=这个故事在讲述中不断扩展，扩展，再扩展。它的广阔已经超出了你的掌控。你发现自己不得不翻阅先前的篇章，以确保人物和细节的连贯性——却意外地撞上了叙事的死胡同——陷入无法挽回的逻辑困境。你以赫拉克勒斯挑战九头蛇的坚定决心开始裁剪。砍去冗余，削减赘述，直到故事血淋淋地躺在你的脚下，却也变得更加完美。第五章完成了。

The ceremony touches even your most cynical swabbie; hardly an eye is left dry.=整个狄坦妮娅都聚集在一起，加入对你们列车员的祈祷和祝福。每个人都收到了自己的长篇祝福。你们也以持久女皇陛下的名义得到了祝福。这个仪式甚至感动了你最愤世嫉俗的人；几乎没有一个人的眼睛是干的。

Themes that had once lurked in the recesses of your mind now rise unbidden to the surface, shaking the last vestiges of your subconscious from their flanks. You finally get the sense that you know what you're doing, and more importantly, where you're going. You sit to write, fingers flying, that shining spectre of an ending blazing like a beacon in your head... until finally, you reach it. The Fourth Canto is complete.=曾经潜伏在你意识深处的主题，如今不请自来，驱散了最后的潜意识余烬。你终于有了方向，知道自己在做什么，更重要的是，知道自己要去往何方。你坐下来，双手在键盘上飞舞，那个闪耀的终点像灯塔般在脑海中燃烧……直到你终于抵达。第四章完成了。

This perfumed haven was intended for thinkers, artists, philosophers and poets. Instead, you enter its main dome to find yourself surrounded by arguing Bohemians and the unfinished shells of buildings in a variety of styles. This signal box has been blasted by weapon fire. Despite the holes, the shattered tiles, the fractured beams, it still has enough structural integrity for you to climb inside without risk. It was made with care, and pride.=这个信号箱被火力摧毁了。尽管有洞，有破碎的瓦砾，有断裂的梁柱，但它的结构仍足够完整，让你毫无风险地爬进去。它是精心制作的，也是自负的。

This stall has a row of wooden ducks that you can shoot at with an air rifle. A child stands alongside it, crying. He has spent all his money and not knocked down one. Time stills to a hush as the great doors close behind you. Silence falls like a curtain over a stage. Your footfall is too loud on the polished marble. \n\nThe Throne is ahead, towering over you in all its splendid immensity. Wilma's had a plan for this situation ready for months. ("Well, I don't sleep well.")  Albrecht is the distraction. His performance as a lost pet rat is riveting. A gaggle of guards argue over who's taking him home for their children. Meanwhile, aided by an unstoppered vial of black smoke courtesy of Vianne, Petronella gets to work with lockpicks. She disarms the triple vial of Cantankeri poison, the rotating tumblers and the serrated unlock mechanism. Cinders barrels through the door.=威尔玛早已为这种情况准备好应对方案。（"嗯，我睡得不好。"）阿尔布雷希特作为诱饵开始行动。他饰演一只走失的宠物老鼠，演技极其逼真。一群守卫争论着谁该把他带回家给孩子当宠物。与此同时，在薇安释放的一瓶黑雾掩护下，佩特罗内拉开始用开锁工具作业。她解除了三重顽固虱毒液瓶、防护转盘与带锯齿的解锁机关。辛德斯猛冲进门。
Wilma's had a plan for this situation ready for months. ("Well, I don't sleep well.") Albrecht is the distraction. His performance as a lost pet rat is riveting. A gaggle of guards argue over who's taking him home for their children. Meanwhile, aided by Angel's distraction – exploding an hours-loom – Petronella gets to work with lockpicks. She disarms the triple vial of Cantankeri poison, the rotating tumblers and the serrated unlock mechanism. Cinders barrels through the door.=威尔玛早已为这种情况准备好应对方案。（"嗯，我睡得不好。"）阿尔布雷希特作为诱饵开始行动。他饰演一只走失的宠物老鼠，演技极其逼真。一群守卫争论着谁该把他带回家给孩子当宠物。与此同时，在安吉炸毁了一台时织机的机智掩护下，佩特罗内拉开始用开锁工具作业。她解除了三重顽固虱毒液瓶、防护转盘与带锯齿的解锁机关。辛德斯猛冲进门。
Window cracks have accreted a filling of moss-green dust. Once-white paint is yellowed and peeling. The signal box possesses a faded dignity, like a beleaguered butler. It was designed with pride – to be part of that great folly, the Isambard Line.=窗户的裂缝里积满了苔藓绿色的灰尘。曾经的白漆变黄、脱落。信号箱的尊严逝去，像个受困的管家。它是设计于自负——作为巨大闹剧的一部分，伊桑巴德航线。
With barely any more support, Melusine Murgatroyd flounces off to invent something capable of properly expressing her annoyance, though it soon turns into some kind of automatic tea-stirrer.=支持者寥寥，梅露齐娜·穆加托德一甩裙摆，去发明某种可以准确表达她恼怒情绪的装置去了，结果那东西很快变成了一个自动搅茶器。
With each step the shrieks of hours being spun backwards resonates through your body, leaving you shaking and worn.=每走一步，时间被倒纺时发出的尖叫都穿透你的身体，让你颤抖不已、疲惫不堪。
With no sun to guide, navigation is almost impossible. From here, you cannot even see Eleutheria's black sun looking down.=没有太阳指引，几乎无法辨别方向。这里看不到俯视厄琉忒里亚的黑日。
With the clock fixed, a team of cleaners begin scrubbing away the painted protests. They will no doubt be back again soon. Port Prosper is getting large enough to chafe against London's more arbitrary demands, especially ones as inconvenient as an Albion Standard Time that bears no relation to how the people here actually carry out their days.=随着时钟的修复，一队清洁工开始擦洗那些涂在各处的抗议标语。毫无疑问的是，它们很快就会再次出现。兴荣港的规模正在不断变大，它已逐渐对伦敦那些武断的要求感到不满，尤其是像阿尔比恩标准时间这样的规定——它与这里人们的实际作息毫无关系，反而带来了诸多不便。
With the clock fixed, the business of the place ticks on. Once a treatment begins, time flows on its own schedule anyway, be clients distracted by the pleasure of massage, the pain of loss, or the confusion of seeing the Empress staring in horror at a mirror, rubbing her face as if in a desperate attempt to rub away something that has been fixed in place for just a little too long.=随着时钟的修复，这里的业务也开始恢复正常。 一旦治疗开始，时间便按照自己的节奏流淌——无论是顾客沉醉于按摩的愉悦，沉浸于失去的痛苦，还是因目睹女皇惊恐地凝视镜中自己、用力揉搓脸庞，仿佛拼命想要抹去某种停留得过久的痕迹而困惑不已。
With the end of the Melee, the questers have separated into dozens of smaller, one-on-one bouts.=混战结束后，探寻者们分成了数十场一对一的决斗。
With the journal finally cracked, you sit back, bleary-eyed. You realise that you've been working for almost two days without food or sleep.=当日志最终被破解时，你睡眼惺忪地向后靠去，意识到自己已经工作了近两天，既没有吃过饭，也没有睡过觉。
With the repelling of the Windward Company forces at New Winchester, the Assembly has moved swiftly to consolidate its gains throughout the Reach. A region wide sweep for London collaborators is ongoing. Port Prosper remains in Company hands, however, and shows no signs of conceding. =随着迎风公司的势力在新温彻斯特被击退，大集会迅速采取行动，巩固其在整个外野的成果。一场针对伦敦合作者的区域性扫荡正在进行。然而，兴荣港仍在公司手中，并没有显示出让步的迹象。 
Within the Palace, you find triumphant re-enactments of doomed partners. Tristan and Isolde, consummating love in life. Werther and Lotte, kissing without tears. Achilles and Patroclus, waging another kind of battle. Don Quixote and Dulcinea, truly alive and noble. Francesca and Paolo, unmurdered as discover each other. Heathcliff and Catherine, actually smiling.=在皇宫里，你会发现注定要失败的伙伴的胜利重演。特里斯坦和伊索尔德，完成了生命中的爱情。维特和洛特，无泪接吻。阿基里斯和帕特洛克罗斯，进行着另一种战斗。堂吉诃德和杜尔西内亚，真正活生生的高贵。弗朗西丝卡和保罗，在发现对方的时候没有受到欺骗。希斯克利夫和凯瑟琳，真正的微笑。
