Words spit like lightning from your fingertips. The typewriter shudders and clanks in protest under the strain. You write in a kind of fury, your mind's eye raging with images of gloating stars and endless burning emptiness – and then you sit back, exhausted, as you realise you've run out of words. The Second Canto is complete.=文字如闪电般从你的指尖迸发。打字机在压力下咔嗒作响。你写作时仿佛陷入了一种狂热，脑海中充满了傲慢的群星与无尽燃烧的虚空——然后，你瘫倒在椅子上，精疲力竭，意识到自己已经写尽所有言辞。第二章完成了。
Work is slow on the new Salon, located just off Valentine Square. The Stone-Faced Sculptor waves away any questions or criticisms, determined only that whatever it turns out to be, it will be the finest example of it in the Reach.=新沙龙的施工进度缓慢，位置紧邻瓦伦丁广场。石面雕塑家对任何质询或批评都不屑一顾，唯一的坚持是——无论最终成品为何，它都将是外野中此类建筑的最佳典范。
Work is underway on the Academy for the Bloody Arts. It is not what they want; but it might be what they need.=血腥艺术学院的建设正在进行中。这或许不是他们想要的，但也许是他们所需要的。
Work is underway on the Apollo Fortress. May it offer the Titanians some protection.=阿波罗要塞的建设正在进行中。愿它能为狄坦妮娅人带来些许庇护。
Work is underway on the Astral Amphitheatre. Epic poetry will no longer be confined to pages alone; they shall be spoken again.=星空露天剧场的建设正在进行中。史诗将不再只被书写，它们将再次被高声吟诵。

Work is underway on the Imperial Cathedral. The hymns they shall sing will ignite future choirs.=帝国大教堂的建设正在进行中。他们将吟唱的圣歌，必将点燃未来的赞颂之声。

You descend to the deepest reaches of London, below even the shrouded slums. This dim realm is called Underlondon, and like most underthings, it is not mentioned in polite company. \n\nOnly the most desperate scavengers venture into Underlondon. Here, the city's rubbish collects in heaps the size of hills; the city's refuse falls like unpleasant, unrelenting rain.=你一路下降至伦敦最深的底部，比迷雾缠绕的贫民窟还要低。这里是一片名为“下伦敦”的幽暗地带，正如所有“下层之物”，它在体面人之间从不被提及。  \n\n只有最绝望的拾荒者才会踏入下伦敦。城市的垃圾在此堆成丘陵，废弃物如连绵不断的恶臭之雨降落于此。

You feel along the passage walls, searching for any sign of the secret doorways used by the Governesses to appear and disappear at will. Your fingers catch on a small groove; when you press the release, the door slides back to reveal an even narrower tunnel. The Debutantes told you the Doors of Night lead directly to the docks. You have found an escape!=你沿着通道的墙壁摸索，寻找宫廷特工们随意隐现时所用的暗门。你的指尖触到一道浅浅的凹槽。你按下机关，门便悄然滑开，露出一条更为狭窄的隧道。闺秀们曾告诉你，夜之门可直通码头。你已找到一条逃生之路！
You feel dizzy. So dizzy.=你感觉头晕。非常晕。

You find a seat among the parishioners, their skin whorled with strange patterns – tentacular calligraphy that might, quite possibly, be flesh.=你在会众之间找了个座位，他们的肌肤盘旋着奇异的纹路——触手般的字迹。那些纹路仿佛真的是肉体的一部分。
You find the Omnivorous Publisher at his paper-drowned desk, flanked by two large gentlemen with shoulders wide as anvils and guns at their hips. "Protection," explains the Publisher, tossing back a glass of whiskey. "Some of your readers are rather... fanatical." He briefly skims your manuscript before handing it to an assistant. =你在堆满文件的办公桌旁找到了包罗万象的出版商，他的两侧站着两个肩膀如铁砧般宽厚、腰间别着枪的彪形大汉。"保护措施，"出版商解释道，一边将手中威士忌一饮而尽，"你的一些读者相当的……狂热。"他快速翻阅你的手稿，然后交给了一名助手。
You find them practising a handstand. They wave at you upside down, balancing on one hand in a surprising display of strength. \n\n"I wonder how they're faring," they say. "Every time we go out, I like to think we get a bit farther. I do miss them dreadfully though."=你看见他们正在练习倒立。倒悬着单手平衡向你挥手，展现出惊人的力量。\n\n"不知他们现下如何，"他们说，"每次出走都以为能走得更远。不过实在想念得紧。"
You find your name among the advertisements, where Sweet Jane hides clues to the identities of those who have displeased her, and prices on their head. You appear in an advertisement for exclusive property in the heights of New Winchester. The number associated with you is very large indeed.=你在广告中找到了自己的名字，这是蜜糖阿简用密文发布那些得罪她的人的身份与赏金的位置。你出现在一则关于新温彻斯特高地的独家房产广告中。跟着你的那个数字真的非常大。
You find your path blocked by a tangle of bleached-white foliage. The Shalimar garden exploded from its greenhouse long ago, feeding furiously on the Sun's light until it swelled to a jungle.=你发现前路被一团漂白的藤蔓阻挡。沙利马尔花园早已横生漫长，在温室外疯狂吞噬阳光，最终膨胀为一片丛林。
You find yourself in a biflected array of caves, all cut from the same cloth.=你发现自己置身于一大堆双向通行的洞穴中，这里所有的洞穴看上去都出自一人的手笔。
You find yourself in a complex series of corridors, all uniform.=你发现自己置身于一系列复杂的石廊之中，这里所有的洞穴看上去都别无二致。

You finish writing the postscript and tear off the final page of the Song of the Sky. Your typewriter immediately falls apart in a clatter of exhausted metal. You rather feel like following suit.=你写完了后记，撕下《天野之歌》的最后一页。你的打字机旋即在一阵疲惫的金属哀鸣中散作一堆零件。你也有些相似的感觉。
You follow the Vagabond past Lustrum's outskirts, into the whirling whiteness beyond. Miles from civilisation, you find a mansion half-buried in snow, perched on a rocky outcrop. The Vagabond wades to the door and slams on the iron knocker.\n\nAfter some time, the door eases open. A doddery butler peers out, his face so downcast it looks half-melted. "Welcome back, sir," says the Tragic Butler wearily. "A letter was delivered in your absence."=你跟随流浪汉穿过伍载镇的边缘地带，走进那片旋转纷飞的白茫茫世界。远离文明数英里之外，你们发现了一座半掩在积雪中的宅邸，孤零零地坐落在岩石突起之上。流浪汉趟雪走到门前，用力敲响了铁制门环。\n\n过了一会儿，门缓缓打开。一位步履蹒跚的老管家探出头来，他的脸忧郁得仿佛已经半融化了。"欢迎回来，先生，"悲惨的管家疲惫地说道，"您不在时，有一封信寄到。"
You follow the shuffling hermit up a hillside, your boots sinking into the mud. Thunder rumbles. Lightning splits the sky.\n\nAt the hilltop you find yourself standing beside a gorge so deep you cannot see the bottom. It's far too wide to leap; the only crossing-point is a bridge.\n\n"The cloak lies across the Falchion Bridge," says the hermit. "Only a worthy quester may cross without slicing himself in two."=你跟着蹒跚的隐士爬上一座山坡，靴子陷入泥中。雷声轰鸣，闪电划破天际。\n\n登上山顶后，你站在一条深不见底的峡谷边。峡谷宽阔得远不能一跃而过，唯一的通路是一座桥。\n\n"斗篷就在弯刀桥的对岸，"隐士说，"唯有真正的勇士，才能穿越此地而不被斩成两半。"

You reach the apex of the Throne. Below you the fearful machinery of Hours; above, the throne of Victoria. Your Aunt hesitates, before reaching for your hand. Together, you approach Her Renewed Majesty.=你抵达王座之巅。脚下是令人生畏的时间机器，上方则是维多利亚的王座。你的姨妈迟疑片刻，然后向你伸出手。你们牵着手一同接近新生的女王陛下。
You recognise the drooping moustache, the mournful eyes. They belong to your old tutor, who died of a fragile heart. He lifts his hands to his face, and his fingers are black with ink.=你认得那下垂的胡须，那哀伤的眼神。那是你一位死于心脏衰弱的导师的面容。他双手掩面，手指上满是墨迹。
You recognise the lady as soon as you enter the room. She had been serving soup at the Round Table or some even lowlier establishment beneath the bridges of New Winchester. Had you made a cutting remark? Perhaps you were less than complimentary about the soup. \n\nWell, no matter; for here she is now, armed with tureens for you to sample. A stained bar-stool has been provided for your comfort.=你一走进房间就认出了那位女士。她曾在圆桌餐馆或新温彻斯特桥下某个更低档的地方为人盛汤。你是否曾对她冷言冷语？也许你对那道汤的评价并不高。\n\n但这无所谓了——她现在就在这里，带着几碗汤供你品尝。她还贴心地为你准备了一张带污渍的吧台高脚椅。

You return to the pier to find guests who had been looking forward to celebrating the Festival of the Sea instead surrounded by half-painted decorations, bunting still in its crates, and tables laden with undercooked meats that ooze when pierced. Worlebury rumbles to the sounds of disappointment, displeasure, and later, intense food poisoning.=你回到码头，发现原本期待庆祝海洋节的宾客们此时却被四处凌乱的半成品装饰、仍在箱子里的彩旗，以及满桌未熟透的肉类围绕着，那些肉在被刺破时还会渗出奇怪汁液。沃伯瑞到处都是失望与不满的抱怨声，这些声音过了一会就变成了严重食物中毒的呻吟。
You return to the train past the now exhausted farmhand, earnestly trying to milk a cow that was milked only a few hours ago and is frankly little happier at the whole affair. He stifles a yawn just long enough to shoot you a foul look.=你返回火车时，与一个筋疲力尽的农场工人擦肩而过，他正认真地尝试给一头几个小时前才挤过的奶牛再次挤奶，看起来他对工作有点烦。他忍住了哈欠，随后狠狠地瞪了你一眼。
You return to your office and write. It's as easy as breathing. The Song of the Sky was a tangled beast at times, but you always knew how it would end, and for once it doesn't surprise you. You get there in no time at all. The Seventh Canto is complete, and you have a final decision to make:=你回到书房，开始写作。这就像呼吸一样自然。《天野之歌》有时曾是头难以驾驭的野兽，但你始终清楚它的终点。而这一次，它没有让你感到意外。你迅速抵达了终点。第七章完成，而你面前，还有最后一个决定。
You return to your waiting. You have read all your books, now. You have listened to your handful of phonograph cylinders. You consider learning the oboe, which you bought in a fit of optimism when furnishing the Gallery.\n\nOutside, thirty more years hurry by. There is a second knock at the door. =你继续等待。你已经读完了所有的书，听完了所有的留声机唱片。你考虑学习双簧管——那是你在布置长廊时，怀着乐观情绪买下的。\n\n在外界，三十年匆匆而逝。门那里传来了第二次敲响。

You ride until your destrier is sheened with sweat, forcing you to stop by a stream and dismount. As it drinks, you unbuckle your armour and rest in the shade of an apple tree.\n\n"An excellent horse. You ride it too hard." \n\nA wizened old hermit has appeared beside your horse, stroking its heaving flanks. "You seek the Cloak of the Lion, yes? I can guide you to its location, if you confess your sins."=你一路策马疾驰，坐骑已汗流浃背，不得不在溪边停下歇息。你下马时，它低头饮水，你则卸下甲胄，坐在苹果树荫下稍作休息。\n\n"是匹好马。不过你骑得太狠了。" \n\n一个面容干瘪的老隐士出现在你坐骑身边，抚摸着它剧烈起伏的肋部。"你是在找狮子斗篷吧？只要你愿意忏悔罪行，我就能引你去往它的所在。"
You ruled your prison corridor like a robber-king. Your punishments were less than you deserved=你如贼王般称霸监狱，你受的惩罚比应得的要少

You search for days, but the wooded mountain stubbornly refuses to yield up a path to the castle. After so much time wasted tramping back and forth, you sit on a tree-stump and wretchedly assess your situation.\n\nExcept... it's not a tree-stump. It's something which is dreadfully, dishearteningly squelchy. And you're no longer at the foot of a mountain, but at the foot of a mountain-size heap of rubbish. Where are you? How did you get here?=你找了好几天，但这座林木茂密的山脉倔强地拒绝显露通往城堡的路径。在徒劳地来回跋涉多日后，你沮丧地坐在一截树墩上，开始审视自己悲惨的处境。  \n\n等等……那不是树墩。那是某种令人不安、令人泄气的湿软之物。而你也不再身处山脚下，而是在一座山一样高的垃圾堆脚下。你在哪里？你是怎么来到这里的？
You search for days, clambering across mountain-sized heaps of abandoned, rusting metal. But you don't find anything that could be said to resemble a queen.=你找了好几天，翻越一座又一座如山般高的废弃锈铁堆。但你没有发现任何能被称作“女王”的存在。

You see the stars beyond Langley Hall when you sleep. Sometimes, in your dreams, you are restless.=当你入眠，你看到兰利厅外的星星。有时，在你的梦中，你的心焦躁不安。

You share a bottle of Wilderness-chilled wine with a troupe of attendants, fresh from a circus performance. You skip pebbles along the mists. At least, you try. Instead of bouncing across the fog, the pebbles excite noises from their depths. After half an hour or so, you manage to string a melody from the sounds.=你沿着迷雾跳过卵石。至少，你在尝试这样做。这些卵石在自己的深度激起了声响，而不是在雾中反弹。大约半小时后，你成功用这种声音组成了一段旋律。
You somehow own the Devourer of Distance twice. This will not go unnoticed.=不知何故，你再度拥有了距离吞噬者。有人会注意到这件事。
You spot a figure masqued in a featureless black, their face covered by a smooth oval of polished obsidian. Who are they?=你看到一个戴着一张光滑的椭圆黑曜石面具的黑衣人。这人是谁？
You squandered it, and enjoyed every moment=你挥霍了它，并享受了每一刻
You stand amidst the gold bones of a sun. They vault and arch like a murdered cathedral, ribs as big as bridges, a curve of skull vast enough to dome all of London.\n\nYou scramble like ants beneath them, discovering that their lengths are twisted and splintered, mangled by some impossible force.\n\nThere is a pressure in your ears, as if unheard thunder was resounding around you.=你们立于太阳的金色骸骨之间。它们如遭屠戮的大教堂穹顶般拱立，肋骨粗若桥梁，头骨弧度之巨足以笼罩整个伦敦。\n\n你们如蝼蚁在其下攀爬，发现这些骸骨尽数扭曲碎裂，似被某种不可名状之力摧折。\n\n耳中嗡鸣不止，恍若有未闻雷鸣在周遭回荡。
You stand beside the stream, occasionally double-checking the number of moons. The stray cat curls up beside you and falls asleep. You have no real idea where to go from here.=你站在溪边，不时确认月亮的数量。流浪猫蜷缩在你脚边睡着了。你根本不知道该往哪里去。
You stare at the half-empty page in panic. For a moment, you fear you've lost your terrible momentum. But then the story catches you in its arms again, and you are a golem, a conduit, a ghost desperate to relay its final message. You write until your fingers are blistered, until the ending catches you by surprise. You recline, sweat-drenched. The Third Canto is complete.=你惊恐地望着那半空的页面。片刻间，你害怕自己失去了那股狂热的冲劲。但随后，故事再度将你吞没，你化身为泥塑的傀儡、传讯的媒介、一个急于倾诉最后信息的幽灵。你不停地写，直到手指起泡，直到故事的终点猝然降临。你瘫倒在椅子上，浑身湿透。第三章完成了。
You step through a cloud of hallucinogenic spores, sneezing colours.=你踏入一团致幻孢子的云雾中，开始打起色彩斑斓的喷嚏。

You stumble over the remnants of some old ruin. Perhaps a castle. Perhaps a temple. Not enough remains of it to hazard a guess.=你在一片断壁残垣中绊了一下。这里也许曾是座城堡，也许曾是座神殿，但已残破到无法辨认。
You successfully collected the Lost Love before the Arbiter's deadline. Now you must return her to Death's Door=你成功在仲裁者的时限前带走了失落的爱人。现在你必须将她送回死亡之门

You take a moment to absorb your surroundings.\n\nThis is not a castle, but a disused and unlit waxwork museum. It's difficult to tell which of the silhouettes in the shadows are wax and which are real. There are straps at the armrests.=你稍作观察，打量四周环境。\n\n这并不是城堡，而是一座废弃且无灯光的蜡像馆。黑暗中难以辨别哪些剪影是蜡像，哪些是真人。一个满脸通红的亨利八世蜡像就在你身旁，表面覆着一层薄薄的灰。\n\n你原以为的盲目王座，其实是一张牙医用椅。扶手上还有束缚用的皮带。

Your friend is lounging on a low couch, his long fingers tapering over a bottle of wine. The room is chilled, which is a fine thing as the bottle is expensive. You talk late into the evening, as he surprises you with several educated guesses about your background, your family, your hobbies and your guilty secrets. \n\nHe confesses a few of his own to you. His own life in the Wilderness, as a captain adrift among the stars. His engine sounds familiar: his boilers started up at strange hours of the night too, his levers stuck at infuriating moments too, and his hull leaked warmth at a surprising rate, just as yours does. When the clock sounds, he smiles. "I shall see you again."=你的朋友懒洋洋地倚在低矮的长沙发上，细长的手指环绕着酒瓶。房间里冷意袭人，这正好衬得那瓶昂贵的酒物有所值。你们一直聊到深夜，他准确地猜出了你的一些背景、家人、兴趣，甚至隐秘的愧疚。\n\n他也向你坦白了一些过往：作为一名在星海间航行的车长，在外野中漂泊的那些岁月。他的机车常在深夜无故启动，他的操纵杆常在紧要关头卡死，他的车壳极易漏风，一切的一切都和你现在这台很像。当钟声响起，他微笑着说道："我们还会再见。"

“Ghastly, isn’t it? I’ve never been able to abide classicists. They’re entirely dogmatic about their Latin, and have never read anything besides Homer and Virgil. And she’s holding court, of course. Just like back when - well. But surely you can see it’s all for nothing? There’s absolutely no way Inanna is here. After all, the whole ruddy point of the legend is she gets up again!”=“真是糟透了，不是吗？我从来就受不了那些古典学者。他们在拉丁文上固执得要命，除了荷马与维吉尔什么都不读。而她还在那里大讲特讲，简直就像当年——呃。不过你总该能看出这根本毫无意义吧？伊南娜怎么可能在这里？她的整个神话核心就是‘她会再度苏醒’啊！”
“I think I’m closer to having conclusive proof. I’d go to the tomb myself, immediately, but I need Summerset’s backing if my findings are to receive any academic legitimacy. As ever in academia, we are beholden to the purse-strings.”=“我想我已经接近确凿的证据了。我恨不得立刻亲自前往那座墓地，但若想让我的研究获得学术认可，仍需夏暮的支持。学术界一如既往地受制于金主。”
“If another snot-nosed professor emeritus in the making explains to me the origin of St John’s cult in Milan, I shall scream. I shall be so cross if he’s right. He has a very nice voice, and that was one of his favourites, once.”=“要是再来一个鼻子不好的教授来向我解释圣约翰崇拜在米兰的起源，我就要崩溃了。如果他说对了，我倒可真要生气。不过老实说，他又唱的《罗兰之歌》倒是挺好听。他嗓音不错，而且那曾是他的最爱之一。”
“It is good to have another face about. I am heartily sick to death of that pair, and not for the first time. They are convinced of such utter rot. Any fool could tell you that this forest is the resting place of Charlemagne. Every legend and romance I’ve read convinces me of it.”=“有新面孔真好。我已经彻底受够那两个了，而且这也不是第一次。他们坚信着一些彻头彻尾的胡言乱语。任何傻瓜都能看出这片森林就是查理曼的长眠之所。每一则传说与骑士故事都让我深信不疑。”
“It's good to see you! Your work in the woods has borne fruit. Summerset has sent me students - and funds - though, of course, not enough to mount another expedition. But that they’re willing to invest at all suggests they’re intrigued by what I’m - we’re - finding here. We shall find her, you and I, and her songs will sound in the heavens again.”=“见到你真高兴！你在森林里的努力结出了果实。夏暮大学派来了学生——还有经费——当然，不够再组织一次探险。但他们愿意投资，说明他们对我——对我们——在此地的发现颇感兴趣。你我定能找到她，让她的歌声再度响彻天穹。”
“I’m glad someone’s decided to talk to me, at least. I’m waiting to see how long it’ll take her acolytes to realise how pig-headed she is. If I have to hear another lecture on the similarities between the greek heroes and the saints, however, I shall scream and scream until Saint John gets out of his grave by himself.”=“至少还有人愿意跟我说话。我倒要看看她的信徒要多久才会意识到她有多固执。可如果我再听到一场讲座，把希腊英雄与圣徒们相提并论，我就要尖叫到圣约翰自己从坟里爬出来为止。”
“Must he carry on so ridiculously? Well, of course he must. God forbid restraint ever enter his stupid head. They’re all prim and proper now,  of course, these awful milk and wafer students – but I know a bacchanal in the making when I see one.”=“他非得这么夸张不可吗？当然，他非得如此。节制这种事从未出现在他那愚蠢的脑袋里。这些讨厌的吃牛奶与圣饼的学生们现在一副正襟危坐的模样，可我一看就知道，这注定要变成一次酒神祭。”
“My evidence, however, is sadly lacking, even if the theory is sound. The queen of heaven is surely situated among the stars. But why is she buried? Well, mythological time is not our time. And here so many things thought impossible reveal themselves to be entirely, magnificently possible. Perhaps this is the Underworld of the ancients.”=“不过，尽管理论合理，我的证据仍然十分匮乏。天后女神无疑栖居于星辰之间，但为何她会被埋葬？唔，神话中的时间并非我们所理解的时间。在此地，许多被认为不可能之事，都能以令人叹服的方式成为现实。也许这里正是古人所说的冥界。”
“My theory isn’t developing as well as I’d like. So many legends speak of Charlemagne departing his castle for a deep well, to there sit until the end of days. His beard grows strong and long, his golden sceptre in his hand, his golden crown on his head. They disagree on the location, however. But if this is not the end of days, what is?”=“我的理论发展得不如我所愿。太多传说都提到查理曼大帝离开城堡，前往一口深井，坐于其间直至末日。他的胡须浓密修长，手持金权杖，头戴金冠。然而，各种传说对地点的说法不一。但若这都不算时日的终结，那什么才算？”
“Summerset’s been so excited by our findings they’ve sent me students! And a little money  – for their upkeep – and for an a proper study of our findings. Not enough for another expedition, of course. Too much material evidence frightens them. The Dean was an archaeologist, I think. He has quite a horror for it now. “=“夏暮对我们的发现激动不已，他们派来了学生！还带来些许资金——用以他们的食宿——还有对我们发现的适当研究。当然，也不足以再发起一场探险。他们对实证材料实在太过惧怕。校长曾是考古学家，估计就是因此才对考古有了阴影。”
“Thank Christ. Someone new. We’ve been trying to reach that tomb on the hill for positively forever. It’s on the far side of the wood, and we’re rather strapped for cash. Summerset’s refusing to send us a penny more for another expedition. They did not appreciate my last monograph. Apparently I brought infamy to the college. I was merely reflecting on our student days.”=“谢天谢地。终于来个新人。我们试图到达那座山丘上的坟冢已经太久了。它在林子的另一边，而我们几乎没钱可用了。夏暮拒绝再拨一分钱，说是我的上一篇专著给学院带来耻辱。但我只是回顾了我们的学生时代罢了。”
“Thanks to you, we have evidence of cultic activity. Which, I’m sure would delight my colleague. But the iconography is distinctively Johannine. If we just had a little more - Summerset might be willing to take this seriously. Perhaps even help fund us through to the grave itself!”=“多亏了你，我们有了邪教活动的证据。我确信，这会使我的同事们感到高兴的。但是，这些图画具有鲜明的约翰福音书风格。如果我们再多掌握一点--萨默塞特可能会愿意认真对待这个问题。也许甚至可以资助我们通过坟墓本身！”
“The evidence we’ve uncovered, while not conclusive, certainly points to a monarch in the early medieval style. His accoutrements classical, his lifestyle and habits decidedly Germanic. Conclusive proof is required, however, before I can justify going to the tomb itself.”=“我们发现的证据虽然尚不确凿，但确实指向一位中世纪早期风格的君主。他的随身器物古典无比，生活方式和嗜好则明显具有日耳曼风格。不过，除非找到决定性证据，否则我还无法说服自己前往那座墓穴。”
“Things aren’t going as well as I’d hoped. According to the Legenda aurea, Saint John is sleeping at Ephesus until the end of the world. I suppose he means to see if he got it right in Revelation. As you may have noticed, we're not in Ephesus. But the apostles were scattered to the ends of the world. If anyone were to find their way to the stars, it would be him."=“事情并没有像我希望的那样发展。据《黄金传说》所说，圣若望将在以弗所沉睡至世界的终结。我想他期望看到自己在启示录中是否说对了。正如你可能注意到的，我们并非在以弗所。但使徒们分散到世界的尽头。如果有人能找到通往恒星的路，那人一定是他。"
“This is intolerable. If they’re not butchering poor Aquinas, they’re drinking themselves senseless. And I cannot imagine the nerve of that - that creature - to lecture anyone on sin and virtue! Leave them alone with enough wine, and they’ll soon forget all about Saint John and the barrow. That’s the whole problem with theology - it doesn’t have to prove anything!”=“我受不了了。他们不是在肢解可怜的阿奎那，就是在把自己灌醉。我真无法理解那家伙怎么还有脸向别人讲授罪行与德性！只要给他们足够的酒，他们很快就会把圣约翰和那座坟冢抛诸脑后。这就是神学的最大问题——它根本不用证明什么！”
“We are so close, I can practically smell him! Will he thank us, I wonder? Accept us as his disciples and let us sit at his feet and hear tales of the death of saints? A cup such as the Catholics use to signify the miracle of communion. I must write to the College. Surely now, they’ll support us.”=“我们已经非常接近了，我几乎可以闻到他的味道！他是否会感谢我们，我想知道？接受我们为他的弟子，让我们坐在他的脚下，听他讲述圣人之死的传说？一个像天主教徒用来象征圣餐奇迹的杯子。我必须给学院写信。现在，他们肯定会支持我们。”
“We have it! The final piece. With all we've found I think Summerset must agree to help fund us to the tomb. Just think, we could place the cup in the king’s hands ourselves!”\n=“我们找到了最后一块拼图！有了发现的一切，我想夏暮学院肯定会同意资助我们前往墓穴。想想吧，我们或许能亲手将圣杯置于王的手中！”\n
“We’ve been here positively months and have nothing to show for it. We’re all but out of funds. I’ve done my bit, making sure the other two can never be taken seriously again  - but we’ve no money for supplies for ventures into the wood. We believe the barrow is the resting place of a legendary monarch. We just can’t agree on which.”=“我们在这里已经好几个月了，毫无所获。经费也快见底了。我已经尽力让那两个永远无法被学界当回事——但我们连再进森林一次的补给都没有。我们都相信那坟冢埋着一位传奇君主，只是各执一词，不知到底是谁。”
“You know I hold you responsible for this sepulchral bacchanalia.” She sniffs. “God, medievalists. Never seen so many corpses still walking about, and I’ve been to the Tomb-Colonies thrice. I simply cannot bear to see that fourth rate practitioner of a third rate discipline triumph.”=“你知道我全怪你让这场死人狂欢节继续下去，”她哼了一声，“中世纪学者，天哪。我去过古墓殖民地三次，都没见过这么多走来走去的尸体。我实在受不了那个三流学科的四流从业者得逞。”
“You see the dividends our work has produced! I have students of my own! Stipends! Grants! Though - between you and me - the College has not sent its best. And not nearly enough funds to cover further exploration. But we are making progress. My Charlemagne will be woken soon enough. And you shall have the credit of helping me find him.”=“你看看我们的工作成效！我有自己的学生了！有奖学金！有研究拨款！虽然——悄悄说——学院派来的可不是最顶尖的，而且拨款远远不够我们继续探索。但我们已经在进展中。我的查理曼很快就会醒来。到时你也能分享发现的荣耀。”
